### 2. Section 1: State Machine - EXACT Types and Transitions

#### 1.1 States Enum

```rust

    Closed,

    Open,
}
```

#### 1.2 Events Enum

```rust
/// Events for the DateRangePicker component.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Event {
    /// Open the popover.
    Open,
    /// Close the popover.
    Close,
    /// Toggle open/closed.
    Toggle,
    /// Calendar has completed range selection.
    SelectRangeComplete {
        /// The selected range.
        range: DateRange
    },
    /// Start date field text changed.
    StartInputChange {
        /// The new value.
        value: String
    },
    /// End date field text changed.
    EndInputChange {
        /// The new value.
        value: String
    },
    /// Focus entered the component.
    FocusIn,
    /// Focus left the component entirely.
    FocusOut,
    /// Key pressed on trigger or within popover.
    KeyDown {
        /// The key that was pressed.
        key: KeyboardKey
    },
}
```

```rust
}
```

#### 1.5 State Transition Table

```rust
impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
    type State = State;
    type Event = Event;
    type Context = Context;
    type Props = Props;
    type Messages = Messages;
    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        ctx: &Self::Context,
        _props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
        if ctx.disabled { return None; }

        match (state, event) {
            // Open transitions
            (State::Closed, Event::Open) | (State::Closed, Event::Toggle) => {
                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Open).apply(|ctx| {
                    ctx.open.set(true);
                }))
            }

            // Close transitions
            (State::Open, Event::Close) | (State::Open, Event::Toggle) => {
                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Closed).apply(|ctx| {
                    ctx.open.set(false);
                }))
            }

            // Range selection from calendar
            (State::Open, Event::SelectRangeComplete { range }) => {
                let range = range.clone();
                let should_close = _props.close_on_select;
                let next_state = if should_close { State::Closed } else { State::Open };
                Some(TransitionPlan::to(next_state).apply(move |ctx| {
                    ctx.start_input_text = format_date(&range.start, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
                    ctx.end_input_text = format_date(&range.end, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
                    ctx.parsed_start = Some(range.start.clone());
                    ctx.parsed_end = Some(range.end.clone());
                    ctx.value.set(Some(range));
                    if should_close {
                        ctx.open.set(false);
                    }
                }))
            }

            // Start field text changed
            (_, Event::StartInputChange { value }) => {
                let text = value.clone();
                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
                    ctx.parsed_start = parse_date(&text, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
                    ctx.start_input_text = text;
                    ctx.active_field = ActiveField::Start;
                    // Update range if both dates are valid
                    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (&ctx.parsed_start, &ctx.parsed_end) {
                        ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(start.clone(), end.clone())));
                    }
                }))
            }

            // End field text changed
            (_, Event::EndInputChange { value }) => {
                let text = value.clone();
                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
                    ctx.parsed_end = parse_date(&text, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
                    ctx.end_input_text = text;
                    ctx.active_field = ActiveField::End;
                    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (&ctx.parsed_start, &ctx.parsed_end) {
                        ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(start.clone(), end.clone())));
                    }
                }))
            }

            // Escape closes popover
            (State::Open, Event::KeyDown { key }) if *key == KeyboardKey::Escape => {
                Self::transition(state, &Event::Close, ctx, _props)
            }

            // ArrowDown opens from closed
            (State::Closed, Event::KeyDown { key }) if *key == KeyboardKey::ArrowDown => {
                Self::transition(state, &Event::Open, ctx, _props)
            }

            // FocusOut closes popover
            (_, Event::FocusOut) if *state == State::Open => {
                Self::transition(state, &Event::Close, ctx, _props)
            }

            _ => None,
        }
    }
}
```

**Guards:**

```rust
fn is_readonly(ctx: &Context) -> bool { ctx.readonly }
```

### 3. Section 2: Connect API and Accessibility

#### 3.1 Anatomy Parts Enum

```rust
#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "date-range-picker"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,
    Label,
    Control,
    StartInput,
    Separator,
    EndInput,
    Trigger,
    ClearTrigger,
    Positioner,
    Content,
    Description,
    ErrorMessage,
    HiddenInput,
}
```

#### 3.2 Parts Table with ARIA Attributes

| Part           | Element       | Key Attributes                                                      |
| -------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Root`         | `<div>`       | `data-ars-scope="date-range-picker"`, `data-ars-part="root"`, `data-ars-state={state}`, optional `data-ars-disabled`, `data-ars-readonly`, `data-ars-required` |
| `Label`        | `<label>`     | `for="{id}-start-input"`, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="label"`, `id={id}-label` |
| `Control`      | `<div>`       | `role="group"`, `aria-labelledby="{id}-label"`, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="control"` |
| `StartInput`   | (DateField)   | Delegates to DateField with `aria-label` from `messages.start_label` |
| `Separator`    | `<span>`      | `aria-hidden="true"`, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="separator"` |
| `EndInput`     | (DateField)   | Delegates to DateField with `aria-label` from `messages.end_label` |
| `Trigger`      | `<button>`    | `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded={open}`, `aria-controls="{id}-content"`, `aria-label` from `messages.trigger_label`, optional `disabled` |
| `ClearTrigger` | `<button>`    | `aria-label` from `messages.clear_label`, `disabled` when no value or disabled |
| `Positioner`   | `<div>`       | Floating UI positioner, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="positioner"` |
| `Content`      | `<div>`       | `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="false"`, `aria-labelledby="{id}-label"`, `id={id}-content`, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="content"` |
| `Description`  | `<div>`       | `id={id}-description`, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="description"` |
| `ErrorMessage` | `<div>`       | `role="alert"`, `id={id}-error-message`, `data-ars-scope`, `data-ars-part="error-message"` |
| `HiddenInput`  | `<input>`     | `type="hidden"`, `name` (from Props), `value="YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD"` (ISO 8601 format) |

#### 3.3 Api Methods - Method Signatures (Exact Quotes)

```rust
/// Attributes for the root element.
pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
    attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id());
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_name());
    if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true); }
    if self.ctx.readonly { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-readonly"), true); }
    if self.ctx.required { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-required"), true); }
    attrs
}

/// Build DateField Props for the start date input.
pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
    date_field::Props {
        id: self.ctx.ids.part("start-input"),
        value: Some(self.ctx.parsed_start.clone()),
        format: self.ctx.format.clone(),
        min: self.ctx.min.clone(),
        max: self.ctx.parsed_end.clone(), // start can't exceed end
        disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
        readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
        aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

/// Build DateField Props for the end date input.
pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
    date_field::Props {
        id: self.ctx.ids.part("end-input"),
        value: Some(self.ctx.parsed_end.clone()),
        format: self.ctx.format.clone(),
        min: self.ctx.parsed_start.clone(), // end can't precede start
        max: self.ctx.max.clone(),
        disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
        readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
        aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

/// Attributes for the trigger element.
pub fn trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Trigger.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
    attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("trigger"));
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.trigger_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::HasPopup), "dialog");
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Expanded), if *self.ctx.open.get() { "true" } else { "false" });
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Controls), self.ctx.ids.part("content"));
    if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true); }
    attrs
}

/// Attributes for the clear trigger element.
pub fn clear_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ClearTrigger.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
    attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.clear_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
    if self.ctx.disabled || self.ctx.value.get().is_none() {
        attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
    }
    attrs
}

/// Attributes for the content element.
pub fn content_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Content.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
    attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("content"));
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "dialog");
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Modal), "false");
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy), self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
    attrs
}

/// Build Calendar Props with range mode enabled.
/// Uses `visible_months: 2` by default so users see start and end month
/// grids side-by-side.
pub fn calendar_props(&self) -> calendar::Props {
    calendar::Props {
        id: self.ctx.ids.part("calendar"),
        is_range: true,
        visible_months: 2,
        page_behavior: PageBehavior::Visible,
        range_start: self.ctx.parsed_start.clone(),
        range_end: self.ctx.parsed_end.clone(),
        min: self.ctx.min.clone(),
        max: self.ctx.max.clone(),
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

/// Screen reader description of the full date range (e.g., "March 1 to March 15").
/// Returns `None` if no complete range is selected.
/// The adapter can use this as an `aria-description` on the root or as a live announcement.
pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {
    let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;
    let start = format_date(&range.start, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);
    let end = format_date(&range.end, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);
    Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(&start, &end, &self.ctx.locale))
}

// Imperative methods
pub fn open(&self)   { (self.send)(Event::Open); }
pub fn close(&self)  { (self.send)(Event::Close); }
pub fn toggle(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Toggle); }
pub fn is_open(&self) -> bool { *self.ctx.open.get() }
pub fn selected_range(&self) -> Option<&DateRange> {
    self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()
}

impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
    type Part = Part;

    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
            Part::Control => self.control_attrs(),
            Part::StartInput => self.start_field_props().into(), // delegates to DateField
            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),
            Part::EndInput => self.end_field_props().into(), // delegates to DateField
            Part::Trigger => self.trigger_attrs(),
            Part::ClearTrigger => self.clear_trigger_attrs(),
            Part::Positioner => self.positioner_attrs(),
            Part::Content => self.content_attrs(),
            Part::Description => self.description_attrs(),
            Part::ErrorMessage => self.error_message_attrs(),
            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),
        }
    }
}
```

#### 3.4 Hidden Input Methods

```rust
/// Attributes for the hidden input element (form submission).
/// When `name` is set, submits range as `"YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD"`.
/// When `start_name`/`end_name` are set, use `start_hidden_input_attrs()`
/// and `end_hidden_input_attrs()` instead for separate form fields.
pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();
    attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
    attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
    if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
    }
    // Submit range as two ISO 8601 dates separated by "/"
    if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
        let iso = format!("{}/{}", range.start.to_iso_string(), range.end.to_iso_string());
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso);
    }
    attrs
}

/// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the start date.
/// Only rendered when `start_name` is set on Props.
pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
    if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
    }
    if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, range.start.to_iso_string());
    }
    attrs
}

/// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the end date.
/// Only rendered when `end_name` is set on Props.
pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
    if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
    }
    if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, range.end.to_iso_string());
    }
    attrs
}
```

### 4. Presets: Date Range Formatting (Section 1.5)

The spec defines date range formatting using ICU4X:

```
1. Date ranges use ICU4X `DateIntervalFormatter`, which handles locale-specific formatting 
   (e.g., English: "May 1--10", German: "1. bis 10. Mai", Arabic: with RTL and native digits).
2. Adapters must NOT construct date ranges by concatenating formatted dates with hardcoded separators.
3. Duration descriptions ("5 days") use ICU4X `PluralRules` for correct pluralization.
4. Prepositions and connectors are part of the formatter output, not separately localized.
```

No preset types are explicitly defined in the spec (e.g., "Last 7 days" presets). The component focuses on manual range selection via Calendar and DateFields, with the Calendar set to `visible_months: 2` by default for side-by-side month grids.

---

### 5. Form Integration (Section 5)

**Two Hidden Input Modes:**

**Mode 1 - Single Range Input (when `name` is set):**
```rust
pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    // Submits range as "YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD"
    // Example: "2024-01-10/2024-01-20"
    if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
        let iso = format!("{}/{}", range.start.to_iso_string(), range.end.to_iso_string());
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso);
    }
}
```

**Mode 2 - Separate Start/End Inputs (when `start_name` and `end_name` are set):**
```rust
pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    // Submits start date as "YYYY-MM-DD" ISO 8601
    if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, range.start.to_iso_string());
    }
}

pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    // Submits end date as "YYYY-MM-DD" ISO 8601
    if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, range.end.to_iso_string());
    }
}
```

**Form Integration Properties:**
- Reset restores `default_value`
- Validation states reflected via `data-ars-invalid` on Root
- `aria-describedby` wires to Description and ErrorMessage parts
- `aria-required` set when `required` is true
- Disabled/readonly propagation from form context per `07-forms.md` S15

---

### 6. Value Sync Semantics (Section 1.7.1: State Ownership and Synchronization)

**Source of Truth:**
The **Calendar** component is the single source of truth for the selected date range, owned by DateRangePicker context:
```rust
struct Context {
    /// Source of truth for the selected range.
    range: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,
}
```

**Synchronization Rules:**

1. **Calendar → DateFields:** When user selects a range via Calendar, the DateFields update to reflect new range
   ```rust
   (State::Open, Event::SelectRangeComplete { range }) => {
       ctx.start_input_text = format_date(&range.start, ...);
       ctx.end_input_text = format_date(&range.end, ...);
       ctx.parsed_start = Some(range.start);
       ctx.parsed_end = Some(range.end);
       ctx.value.set(Some(range));
   }
   ```

2. **DateFields → Calendar:** When user edits a date via DateField, the Calendar updates to reflect new value
   ```rust
   (_, Event::StartInputChange { value }) => {
       ctx.parsed_start = parse_date(&text, ...);
       if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (&ctx.parsed_start, &ctx.parsed_end) {
           ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(start, end)));
       }
   }
   ```

3. **Controlled Mode** (`value` prop set): Both Calendar and DateFields reflect controlled value. User edits trigger `on_change` but do NOT update internal state.

4. **Uncontrolled Mode:** Internal `range` is source of truth. Both Calendar and DateFields read from and write to it.

**Conflict Resolution:**
- If DateField edit produces invalid range (start > end), edit is accepted but range marked invalid via `ctx.is_invalid = true`
- Calendar highlights invalid range with visual indicator
- `on_change` fires with invalid range; consumer decides whether to correct it

### 7. ComponentIds and data-ars-* Attributes (Integrated into Section 3.2 above)

**Key id-related patterns:**
- **Root ID:** `{id}` (from Props)
- **Label ID:** `{id}-label`
- **Control:** No explicit ID, groups StartInput, Separator, EndInput
- **StartInput ID:** `{id}-start-input`
- **EndInput ID:** `{id}-end-input`
- **Trigger ID:** `{id}-trigger`
- **Content ID:** `{id}-content` (used by trigger's `aria-controls`)
- **Description ID:** `{id}-description`
- **ErrorMessage ID:** `{id}-error-message`
- **Calendar ID:** `{id}-calendar`

**data-ars-* attributes on Root:**
- `data-ars-scope="date-range-picker"` (from Part::Root.data_attrs())
- `data-ars-part="root"`
- `data-ars-state="closed" | "open"` (dynamic)
- `data-ars-disabled="true"` (conditional)
- `data-ars-readonly="true"` (conditional)
- `data-ars-required="true"` (conditional)

### 8. Anatomy / Parts List

**13 Parts (from Part enum):**

1. **Root** - Container div with scope and state attributes
2. **Label** - Label element linked to start input
3. **Control** - Group container for the two date fields, separator, and buttons
4. **StartInput** - DateField instance for start date
5. **Separator** - Visual separator between start and end (aria-hidden)
6. **EndInput** - DateField instance for end date
7. **Trigger** - Button to open/close calendar popover
8. **ClearTrigger** - Button to clear selected range
9. **Positioner** - Floating UI container
10. **Content** - Dialog wrapper for calendar
11. **Description** - Help text element
12. **ErrorMessage** - Validation error alert
13. **HiddenInput** - Form submission input (ISO 8601 range value)

**Tree Structure:**
```
Root
├─ Label
├─ Control
│  ├─ StartInput (DateField)
│  ├─ Separator
│  ├─ EndInput (DateField)
│  ├─ Trigger
│  └─ ClearTrigger
├─ Positioner
│  └─ Content (role="dialog")
│     └─ Calendar (is_range=true, visible_months=2)
├─ Description
├─ ErrorMessage
└─ HiddenInput
```

---

## Summary

The DateRangePicker is a **stateful** component composing two DateField instances with a Calendar (in range mode) inside a popover. It manages state transitions (Closed/Open), synchronizes values bidirectionally between DateFields and Calendar, and provides dual form submission modes (single range or separate start/end ISO 8601 inputs). All parts use the `data-ars-scope="date-range-picker"` marking convention with locale-aware formatting via ICU4X.

```rust
    Open,                      // Open the calendar popover

    InputChange { value: String },      // Text input value changed (user typing)
    FocusIn,                   // Focus entered the input field
    FocusOut,                  // Focus left the date picker entirely
    KeyDown { key: KeyboardKey },       // Keyboard event on input or trigger

}
```

```rust
    FocusCalendar,             // Move focus into the embedded calendar grid
    RestoreFocusToTrigger,     // Return focus to the trigger button
```

### 3. Transition Logic Highlights

**Key state transitions** (lines 952–1247):

1. **Open/Close Lifecycle** — `open_request()` helper (lines 757–807):
   - When `open` is **controlled** (`props.open.is_some()`): Does NOT mutate state; records request in `requested_open` and `requested_focus`, emits `OpenChange`, lets parent reconcile via `SyncProps`
   - When **uncontrolled**: Transitions state and emits focus effect immediately
   - Returns `None` if already in requested state (no-op)

2. **Date Selection** (`Event::SelectDate`, lines 1029–1121):
   - Validates date is in range and available (defense-in-depth)
   - Updates value, input text, and `parsed_date`
   - Closes popover if `close_on_select && state == Open`
   - Emits `ValueChange` only if value actually changes
   - Controlled `open`: Emits close request for parent to reconcile; focus restore deferred to `SyncProps`
   - Uncontrolled: State transitions immediately; focus effect fires immediately

3. **Input Parsing** (`Event::InputChange`, lines 1123–1208):
   - Classifies input as Valid, CompleteInvalid, or Partial (via `classify_input()`)
   - Valid complete date in range → commits
   - Complete invalid date (e.g., `02/30/2024`) → clears value
   - Empty → clears
   - Partial (still typing) → preserves committed value, shows typed text (uncontrolled) or normalized text (controlled)
   - Uses `normalize_digits()` to handle native decimal digits (Arabic-Indic, etc.)

4. **Controlled Props Sync** (`Event::SyncProps`, lines 960–1007):
   - Re-applies controlled `value` via `Bindable::sync_controlled()`
   - Updates cached fields: `min`, `max`, `today`, `disabled`, `readonly`, `open_on_click`, `name`, `required`, `is_rtl`, `format`
   - **Preserves input text** when display value doesn't change (unrelated prop updates don't clobber partial entries)
   - Processes pending focus intent from user event: `requested_focus` → effect
   - For controlled `open` changes: transitions state, applies pending focus or per-direction default (calendar on open, trigger on close)

5. **Disabled/Readonly Guards** (line 1009–1011):
   - `SyncProps` flows through even when disabled (so disabling can be cleared)
   - All other events return `None` when disabled
   - `SelectDate` and `InputChange` also blocked when `readonly`
   - `Open`, `FocusIn` **not blocked** when readonly (viewing is allowed)

6. **Keyboard Handling** (lines 1224–1242):
   - **Escape** when open: closes and restores focus to trigger
   - **ArrowDown** when closed: opens and focuses calendar
   - **ArrowDown** when open: moves focus into calendar grid (no state change)

7. **Focus Management** (lines 1210–1222):
   - `FocusIn` on input: opens if `open_on_click && closed` (no focus effect—user is already in input)
   - `FocusOut`: closes popover (emits `OpenChange`, no focus effect—focus already left)
   - Recorded via `OpenFocus` enum (lines 724–744) to decouple user intent from controlled-prop reconciliation timing

---

### 4. Format/Parse System

**Default format** (lines 424–452):
```rust
fn default_format_for_locale(locale: &Locale) -> String
```
- Uses `ars_i18n::date_order()` for field order (MMddyyyy, ddMMyyyy, yyyyMMdd per locale)
- Uses `ars_i18n::date_field_separator()` for separator (`.` for German/Korean, `/` elsewhere)
- Returns e.g. `"MM/dd/yyyy"` for en-US, `"dd.MM.yyyy"` for de-DE, `"yyyy/MM/dd"` for ja-JP

**Format parsing** (lines 460–481):
```rust
fn parse_format(format: &str) -> (char, [DateField; 3])
```
- Extracts separator (first non-alphabetic char, default `/`)
- Extracts field order from first char of each token (`y`→year, `M`→month, `d`→day)
- Falls back to MMddyyyy if not 3 tokens

**Formatting** (lines 487–513):
```rust
fn format_date(date: &CalendarDate, format: &str) -> String
```
- Zero-pads month/day to 2 digits, year to 4, in pattern's field order
- Example: `2024-03-15` with `"MM/dd/yyyy"` → `"03/15/2024"`

**Parsing** (lines 521–548):
```rust
fn parse_date(text: &str, format: &str) -> Option<CalendarDate>
```
- Transliterates native digits to ASCII via `ars_i18n::normalize_digits()`
- Splits on format separator, validates 3 numeric tokens
- Constructs `CalendarDate::new_gregorian(year, month, day)`, returns `None` if invalid

**Input classification** (lines 550–589):
```rust
enum InputClass { Valid(CalendarDate), CompleteInvalid, Partial }
fn classify_input(text: &str, format: &str) -> InputClass
```
- "Complete" = exactly 3 numeric fields after normalization
- Valid complete → accepts commit
- Invalid complete (real numbers but bad date) → commits a clear
- Partial (non-numeric, too few fields) → leaves committed value untouched

### 5. Compose API: Calendar Props

**Method** (lines 1690–1707):
```rust
pub fn calendar_props(&self) -> calendar::Props {
    calendar::Props {
        id: format!("{}-calendar", self.ctx.ids.id()),
        value: Some(self.ctx.value.get().clone()),
        min: self.ctx.min.clone(),
        max: self.ctx.max.clone(),
        disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
        readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
        is_date_unavailable: self.props.is_date_unavailable.clone(),
        is_rtl: self.ctx.is_rtl,
        visible_months: self.props.visible_months,
        today: self.ctx.today.clone(),
        ..calendar::Props::default()
    }
}
```

**Composition pattern:**
- DatePicker creates a Calendar machine **inside** the popover content
- Wires Calendar's `SelectDate` event back to DatePicker as `Event::SelectDate`
- Forwards: value, min/max, disabled/readonly, is_date_unavailable, is_rtl, visible_months, today
- Calendar owns month/year navigation, grid rendering, focus management; DatePicker owns popover open/close, input field, form integration

**Part Enum** (lines 656–692):

```rust
#[derive(ars_core::ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "date-picker"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,           // Outermost container
    Label,          // Associated label pointing to input
    Control,        // Wrapper around input and trigger button
    Input,          // Date text input
    Trigger,        // Calendar icon button that toggles popover
    ClearTrigger,   // Button that clears selected date; hidden when empty
    Positioner,     // Floating positioner for popover content
    Content,        // Popover content (role="dialog") containing the embedded Calendar
    Description,    // Optional help text
    ErrorMessage,   // Validation error text (role="alert")
    HiddenInput,    // Hidden input carrying ISO date string for form submission
}
```

**Attrs methods in Api<'a>**:
- `root_attrs()` — Sets scope, part, id, state name, disabled/readonly flags, RTL
- `label_attrs()` — Points to input via `for` attribute
- `control_attrs()` — Container for input + trigger
- `input_attrs()` (lines 1346–1427) — text input with:

  - `aria-labelledby={label-id}`, `aria-describedby={description|error-id}`
  - `aria-description` with formatted selected date (e.g., "Selected date: 03/15/2024")

- `trigger_attrs()` — Calendar button, `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded`, disabled only (not readonly)
- `clear_trigger_attrs()` — Hidden when empty, disabled when disabled/readonly
- `positioner_attrs()` — Hidden when closed
- `content_attrs()` — `role="dialog"`, `aria-label={content_label_message}`
- `description_attrs()`, `error_message_attrs()` — Supporting text containers

### 7. Form Integration

**Hidden Input Pattern** (lines 1568–1604):
```rust
pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    // ... scope, part setup ...
    
    if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {
        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
    }
    
    if self.ctx.disabled {
        attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);  // Disabled form controls don't submit
    }
    
    let value = self
        .ctx
        .value
        .get()
        .as_ref()
        .map(CalendarDate::to_iso8601)
        .unwrap_or_default();
    
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);
    attrs
}
```

**Contract:**
- Always present, always carries ISO 8601 string (e.g., `"2024-12-25"`)
- Name comes from props for form submission
- Disabled state mirrors the component
- Decoupled from visible input (which uses locale-aware format)

```rust
#[test]
fn sync_props_applies_controlled_value() {
    let mut svc = service_with(Props { value: Some(None), ..props() }, en_us());
    drop(svc.set_props(Props { value: Some(Some(date(2024, 3, 15))), ..props() }));

    assert_eq!(svc.context().input_text, "03/15/2024");
}

#[test]
fn sync_props_unrelated_change_preserves_typed_text() {
    // Partial entry survives prop change that doesn't touch value/format
    drop(svc.send(Event::InputChange { value: String::from("03/15") }));
    drop(svc.set_props(Props { invalid: true, ..props() }));
    assert_eq!(svc.context().input_text, "03/15");
}
```

### 9. Connect API Methods

**Typed handlers** (lines 1606–1680):
```rust
pub fn on_trigger_click(&self) {
    (self.send)(Event::Toggle);
}

pub fn on_trigger_keydown(&self, data: &KeyboardEventData) -> bool {
    match data.key {
        KeyboardKey::Enter | KeyboardKey::Space => {
            (self.send)(Event::Toggle);
            true  // Consumed; adapter suppresses native button click
        }
        KeyboardKey::ArrowDown => {
            (self.send)(Event::Open);
            true
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

pub fn on_clear_trigger_click(&self) {
    (self.send)(Event::InputChange { value: String::new() });
}

pub fn on_input_change(&self, value: &str) {
    (self.send)(Event::InputChange { value: value.to_string() });
}

pub fn on_input_keydown(&self, key: KeyboardKey) {
    (self.send)(Event::KeyDown { key });
}

pub fn on_focusin(&self) {
    (self.send)(Event::FocusIn);
}

pub fn on_focusout(&self, focus_leaving_component: bool) {
    if focus_leaving_component {
        (self.send)(Event::FocusOut);
    }
}

pub fn on_content_keydown(&self, data: &KeyboardEventData) {
    if data.key == KeyboardKey::Escape {
        (self.send)(Event::Close);
    }
}
```

**Programmatic control**:
```rust
pub fn open(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Open); }
pub fn close(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Close); }
pub fn toggle(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Toggle); }
```

```rust
pub fn selected_date(&self) -> Option<&CalendarDate> { self.ctx.value.get().as_ref() }
pub fn formatted_value(&self) -> String { self.ctx.formatted_value() }
```

### 12. Key Design Patterns for DateRangePicker

Based on this analysis, a DateRangePicker sibling component should:

1. **Extend to two dates:**
   - Change `value: Bindable<Option<CalendarDate>>` → `Bindable<Option<DateRange>>`
   - Add `first_date`, `second_date`, `selecting_start` to Context (track two-step selection)
   - Use `RangeCalendar` instead of `Calendar` (already exists at `/range_calendar/`)

2. **Format/parse two dates:**
   - Extend `format_date()` and `parse_date()` to handle separator between start–end (e.g., `"03/15/2024 – 04/20/2024"` with a range separator)
   - Or display as `"Start: 03/15/2024, End: 04/20/2024"`

3. **Reuse composition pattern:**
   - `calendar_props()` becomes `range_calendar_props()` forwarding value, min/max, constraints
   - Bind `RangeCalendar`'s events back to DateRangePicker events

4. **Input field behavior:**
   - Separate input field for start & end (or one combined field)
   - Parsing must extract both dates from the text
   - `InputChange` classifies as before (Valid, CompleteInvalid, Partial)

5. **Controlled/uncontrolled:**
   - Mirror DatePicker's `Bindable` sync and `SyncProps` flow
   - `on_range_change` callback mirroring `on_value_change`

6. **Test structure:**
   - Mirror test suite (initial state, open/close, selection, parsing, controlled sync, ARIA, snapshots)
   - Add tests for two-step selection flow, range constraints (min/max range length)

### 3. **DATERANGEFIELD: PUBLIC API & ANATOMY PARTS**

#### **ConnectApi Surface (Api<'a> struct methods):**

- `end_hidden_input_attrs() -> AttrMap` — separate hidden input for end date

**Data Getters:**
- `separator_text() -> String` — separator text rendered between fields (e.g., " – ")
- `range_description() -> Option<String>` — screen-reader description of full range (e.g., "March 1, 2025 to March 15, 2025")

- `end_field_props() -> date_field::Props` — built child props for end field

- `active_field() -> Option<ActiveField>` — which field has focus (if any)

- `is_invalid() -> bool` — whether current range violates min/max bounds

**Event Dispatch (called by adapters):**
- `focus_start()` — focus moved to start field
- `focus_end()` — focus moved to end field
- `blur()` — focus left entire component
- `set_range(&self, range: Option<DateRange>)` — set full range programmatically
- `set_start_value(&self, date: Option<CalendarDate>)` — report start-field change
- `set_end_value(&self, date: Option<CalendarDate>)` — report end-field change

### 5. **DATERANGEFIELD: TRANSITION FUNCTION STRUCTURE**

The machine implements `ars_core::Machine` with transition logic in the `transition()` method (lines 466–537):

```rust
fn transition(
    _state: &Self::State,
    event: &Self::Event,
    ctx: &Self::Context,
    _props: &Self::Props,
) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>
```

**Key characteristics:**
- **Guard condition:** `SyncProps` must process even when disabled; all other events blocked if `ctx.disabled`
- **Context-only transitions:** Most transitions only modify context (no state change):
  - `SyncProps` → calls `sync_props()` helper
  - `StartValueChange` → calls `apply_start_change()` → `recompute_range()`
  - `EndValueChange` → calls `apply_end_change()` → `recompute_range()`
  - `SetRange` → direct value assignment

- **State transitions with context updates:**
  - `FocusStart` → `State::StartFocused` + set `active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start)`
  - `FocusEnd` → `State::EndFocused` + set `active_field = Some(ActiveField::End)`
  - `BlurAll` → `State::Idle` + set `active_field = None`

- **Normalization logic:** `recompute_range()` (lines 611–628) normalizes the range so `start <= end`:
  ```rust
  fn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {
      match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {
          (Some(start), Some(end)) => match DateRange::normalized(start, end) {
              Some(range) => {
                  ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());
                  ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());
                  ctx.value.set(Some(range));
              }
              None => ctx.value.set(None), // Dates not comparable
          },
          _ => ctx.value.set(None), // Incomplete range
      }
  }
  ```

```rust
    pub is_date_unavailable: Option<IsDateUnavailableFn>,

    /// Explicit override of the locale default first day of week.
    pub first_day_of_week: Option<Weekday>,

    /// Whether the head row exposes ISO week numbers.
    pub show_week_numbers: bool,

    /// Right-to-left layout direction.
    pub is_rtl: bool,

    /// Number of months displayed side-by-side. Zero falls back to the default.
    pub visible_months: usize,

    /// Controls navigation step size.
    pub page_behavior: PageBehavior,

    /// The "today" date supplied by the adapter for determinism.
    pub today: CalendarDate,

    /// Whether a range with identical start and end dates is valid.
    pub allow_single_date_range: bool,

    /// Minimum inclusive range length in calendar days.
    pub min_range_days: Option<u32>,

    /// Maximum inclusive range length in calendar days.
    pub max_range_days: Option<u32>,
}
```

### 3. **RANGECALENDAR: PUBLIC API & ANATOMY PARTS**

#### **ConnectApi Surface (Api<'a> struct methods):**

**Root & Header Attributes:**
- `root_attrs() -> AttrMap` — outer root element
- `header_attrs() -> AttrMap` — header container for navigation and heading
- `prev_trigger_attrs() -> AttrMap` — previous-month/page button
- `next_trigger_attrs() -> AttrMap` — next-month/page button
- `heading_attrs() -> AttrMap` — main heading with live-region attributes

**Grid Attributes:**
- `grid_attrs() -> AttrMap` — attributes for first visible grid
- `grid_attrs_for(offset: usize) -> AttrMap` — attributes for grid at offset
- `grid_group_attrs() -> AttrMap` — wrapper for multi-grid display
- `head_row_attrs() -> AttrMap` — weekday header row
- `head_cell_attrs(weekday: Weekday) -> AttrMap` — individual weekday header
- `row_attrs(week_index: usize) -> AttrMap` — week row with index
- `cell_attrs(date: &CalendarDate) -> AttrMap` — date cell
- `cell_attrs_for(date: &CalendarDate, offset: usize) -> AttrMap` — date cell at offset
- `cell_trigger_attrs(date: &CalendarDate) -> AttrMap` — interactive trigger inside cell
- `cell_trigger_attrs_for(date: &CalendarDate, offset: usize) -> AttrMap` — trigger at offset

**Text & Labels:**
- `heading_text() -> String` — heading for first visible month
- `heading_text_for(offset: usize) -> String` — heading for month at offset
- `range_heading_text() -> String` — heading spanning all visible months
- `week_day_labels() -> Vec<(Weekday, String)>` — ordered weekday pairs for head row
- `cell_label(date: &CalendarDate) -> String` — aria-label for cell with suffixes

**State Queries:**
- `is_in_range(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date falls inside confirmed range
- `is_range_start(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date is confirmed range start
- `is_range_end(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date is confirmed range end
- `is_in_hover_range(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date falls in pending hover preview range
- `is_anchor(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date is pending range anchor
- `is_today(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date equals configured today
- `is_disabled(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date is disabled (min/max/global)
- `is_unavailable(date: &CalendarDate) -> bool` — date is marked unavailable
- `is_outside_month_for(date: &CalendarDate, offset: usize) -> bool` — date outside month at offset
- `is_focused() -> bool` — grid has focus
- `today() -> &CalendarDate` — configured today date
- `focused_date() -> &CalendarDate` — current focused date

**Navigation & Display:**
- `visible_month_count() -> usize` — number of visible months
- `month_offsets() -> Range<usize>` — iterator over visible month offsets
- `show_week_numbers() -> bool` — whether week numbers should render
- `weeks() -> Vec<[CalendarDate; 7]>` — 6-week grid for first visible month
- `weeks_for(offset: usize) -> Vec<[CalendarDate; 7]>` — 6-week grid for month at offset
- `is_prev_disabled() -> bool` — previous trigger disabled
- `is_next_disabled() -> bool` — next trigger disabled

**Event Dispatch (called by adapters):**
- `on_cell_click(date: CalendarDate)` → `Event::SelectDate`
- `on_cell_hover(date: CalendarDate)` → `Event::HoverDate`
- `on_grid_mouseleave()` → `Event::HoverEnd`
- `on_grid_focusin()` → `Event::FocusIn`
- `on_grid_focusout(focus_leaving_grid: bool)` → `Event::FocusOut`
- `on_grid_keydown(key: KeyboardKey, shift: bool)` → `Event::KeyDown`
- `on_prev_click()` → `Event::PrevMonth`
- `on_next_click()` → `Event::NextMonth`

#### **Anatomy Parts:**
```rust
#[derive(ars_core::ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "range-calendar"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,                              // Outer component root
    Header,                            // Header container for navigation/heading
    PrevTrigger,                       // Previous-month/page button
    NextTrigger,                       // Next-month/page button
    Heading,                           // Textual month/year heading
    Grid,                              // Grid wrapper for visible month
    GridGroup,                         // Wrapper grouping multiple visible grids
    HeadRow,                           // Header row of weekday labels
    HeadCell { day: Weekday },         // Individual weekday header cell
    Row { week_index: usize },         // Row of day cells
    Cell {
        date: CalendarDate,
        offset: usize,
    },                                 // Grid cell wrapping date trigger
    CellTrigger {
        date: CalendarDate,
        offset: usize,
    },                                 // Interactive trigger inside cell
}
```

### 5. **RANGECALENDAR: TRANSITION FUNCTION STRUCTURE**

The machine implements `ars_core::Machine` with transition logic in the `transition()` method (lines 1046–1165):

```rust
fn transition(
    _state: &Self::State,
    event: &Self::Event,
    ctx: &Self::Context,
    _props: &Self::Props,
) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>
```

**Key characteristics:**

- **Guard conditions:**
  - `FocusOut` always transitions to `State::Idle` (line 1052–1054)
  - `SyncProps` always processes (line 1056–1062)
  - All other events blocked if `ctx.disabled` (line 1064–1066)
  - `readonly` guards selection but not navigation/focus

- **Two-step range selection:**
  1. First click: anchors the start point, pending state (`anchor_date = Some(date)`)
  2. Second click: completes range, validates length/bounds, clears anchor
  - Helper function: `apply_select_date()` (lines 1246–1289)

- **State transitions:**
  - `FocusIn` → `State::Focused`
  - `FocusOut` → `State::Idle`
  - All other transitions may transition to `State::Focused` with context changes

- **Navigation logic:**
  - `NextMonth`/`PrevMonth` → `month_step_plan()` with `step_for_page_behavior()`
  - `NextYear`/`PrevYear` → ±12 month step
  - `SetMonth`/`SetYear` → jump navigation with validation
  - `KeyDown` → handled by `handle_keydown()` function (lines 1340–1423)

- **Keyboard navigation:**
  - Arrow keys: Day navigation (respects RTL)
  - Page Up/Page Down: Month navigation
  - Shift+Page Up/Down: Year navigation (±12 months)
  - Home/End: Week boundary navigation
  - Enter/Space: Selection

- **Effects generated:**
  - `AnnounceMonth` on month/year navigation
  - `AnnounceRangeStart` after selecting first endpoint
  - `AnnounceRangeComplete` after completing range

**Example: Select Date Transition (lines 1246–1289):**
```rust
fn apply_select_date(ctx: &Context, date: CalendarDate) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Machine>> {
    if ctx.readonly || ctx.is_date_disabled(&date) || ctx.is_date_unavailable(&date) {
        return None;
    }

    if let Some(anchor) = &ctx.anchor_date {
        // Second click: try to complete range
        let range = DateRange::normalized(anchor.clone(), date.clone())?;
        if !range_is_selectable(ctx, &range) {
            // Range violates constraints; just update focus
            return Some(
                TransitionPlan::to(State::Focused).apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
                    ctx.focused_date = date;
                    ctx.sync_visible_to_focused();
                }),
            );
        }

        // Valid range: clear anchor, set value, announce
        Some(
            TransitionPlan::to(State::Focused)
                .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
                    ctx.value.set(Some(range));
                    ctx.anchor_date = None;
                    ctx.hovering_date = None;
                    ctx.focused_date = date;
                    ctx.sync_visible_to_focused();
                })
                .with_effect(ars_core::PendingEffect::named(
                    Effect::AnnounceRangeComplete,
                )),
        )
    } else {
        // First click: anchor the date
        Some(
            TransitionPlan::to(State::Focused)
                .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
                    ctx.anchor_date = Some(date.clone());
                    ctx.hovering_date = None;
                    ctx.focused_date = date;
                    ctx.value.set(None);
                })
                .with_effect(ars_core::PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceRangeStart)),
        )
    }
}
```

187	
188	### 1.5 Date Range Formatting
189	
190	1. Date ranges use ICU4X `DateIntervalFormatter`, which handles locale-specific formatting (e.g., English: "May 1--10", German: "1. bis 10. Mai", Arabic: with RTL and native digits).
191	2. Adapters must NOT construct date ranges by concatenating formatted dates with hardcoded separators.
192	3. Duration descriptions ("5 days") use ICU4X `PluralRules` for correct pluralization.
193	4. Prepositions and connectors are part of the formatter output, not separately localized.
194	
195	### 1.6 Guards
196	
197	```rust
198	fn is_disabled(ctx: &Context) -> bool { ctx.disabled }
199	fn is_readonly(ctx: &Context) -> bool { ctx.readonly }
200	```
201	
202	### 1.7 Composition Pattern
203	
204	Event bridging from child components to DateRangePicker:
205	
206	```text
207	Calendar.SelectRangeEnd -> DateRangePicker.SelectRangeComplete { range }
208	StartDateField.SetValue -> DateRangePicker.StartInputChange { value } (parse, update range.start)
209	EndDateField.SetValue   -> DateRangePicker.EndInputChange { value }   (parse, update range.end)
210	```
211	
212	> **Multi-month recommendation**: DateRangePicker uses `visible_months: 2` by default so users see start and end month grids side-by-side.
213	
214	#### 1.7.1 State Ownership and Synchronization
215	
216	The **Calendar** component is the single source of truth for the selected date range.
217	
218	```rust
219	// DateRangePicker context owns the canonical range
220	struct Context {
221	    /// Source of truth for the selected range.
222	    range: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,
223	    // ...
224	}
225	```
226	
227	**Sync Rules:**
228	
229	1. When the user selects a range via the Calendar, the DateFields update to reflect the new range.
230	2. When the user edits a date via a DateField, the Calendar updates to reflect the new value.
231	3. In controlled mode (`value` prop set), both Calendar and DateFields reflect the controlled value. User edits trigger `on_change` but do not update internal state.
232	4. In uncontrolled mode, internal `range` is the source of truth. Both Calendar and DateFields read from and write to it.
233	
234	**Conflict Resolution:**
235	
236	- If a DateField edit produces an invalid range (start > end), the edit is accepted but the range is marked invalid via `ctx.is_invalid = true`.
237	- The Calendar highlights the invalid range with a visual indicator.
238	- `on_change` fires with the invalid range; the consumer decides whether to correct it.
239	
240	### 1.8 Full Machine Implementation
241	
242	```rust
243	/// Machine for the DateRangePicker component.
244	pub struct Machine;
245	
246	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
247	    type State = State;
248	    type Event = Event;
249	    type Context = Context;
250	    type Props = Props;
251	    type Messages = Messages;
252	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
253	
254	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
255	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
256	        let messages = messages.clone();
257	
258	        let (start_text, end_text, parsed_start, parsed_end) = match &props.default_value {
259	            Some(range) => (
260	                format_date(&range.start, &props.format, &locale),
261	                format_date(&range.end, &props.format, &locale),
262	                Some(range.start.clone()),
263	                Some(range.end.clone()),
264	            ),
265	            None => (String::new(), String::new(), None, None),
266	        };
267	
268	        let ctx = Context {
269	            value: match &props.value {
270	                Some(v) => Bindable::controlled(v.clone()),
271	                None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone()),
272	            },
273	            open: Bindable::uncontrolled(false),
274	            start_input_text: start_text,
275	            end_input_text: end_text,
276	            parsed_start,
277	            parsed_end,
278	            active_field: ActiveField::Start,
279	            min: props.min.clone(),
280	            max: props.max.clone(),
281	            locale,
282	            messages,
283	            format: props.format.clone(),
284	            disabled: props.disabled,
285	            readonly: props.readonly,
286	            required: props.required,
287	            name: props.name.clone(),
288	            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),
289	            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),
290	            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
291	        };
292	        (State::Closed, ctx)
293	    }
294	
295	    fn transition(
296	        state: &Self::State,
297	        event: &Self::Event,
298	        ctx: &Self::Context,
299	        _props: &Self::Props,
300	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
301	        if ctx.disabled { return None; }
302	
303	        match (state, event) {
304	            (State::Closed, Event::Open) | (State::Closed, Event::Toggle) => {
305	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Open).apply(|ctx| {
306	                    ctx.open.set(true);
307	                }))
308	            }
309	
310	            (State::Open, Event::Close) | (State::Open, Event::Toggle) => {
311	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Closed).apply(|ctx| {
312	                    ctx.open.set(false);
313	                }))
314	            }
315	
316	            (State::Open, Event::SelectRangeComplete { range }) => {
317	                let range = range.clone();
318	                let should_close = _props.close_on_select;
319	                let next_state = if should_close { State::Closed } else { State::Open };
320	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(next_state).apply(move |ctx| {
321	                    ctx.start_input_text = format_date(&range.start, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
322	                    ctx.end_input_text = format_date(&range.end, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
323	                    ctx.parsed_start = Some(range.start.clone());
324	                    ctx.parsed_end = Some(range.end.clone());
325	                    ctx.value.set(Some(range));
326	                    if should_close {
327	                        ctx.open.set(false);
328	                    }
329	                }))
330	            }
331	
332	            (_, Event::StartInputChange { value }) => {
333	                let text = value.clone();
334	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
335	                    ctx.parsed_start = parse_date(&text, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
336	                    ctx.start_input_text = text;
337	                    ctx.active_field = ActiveField::Start;
338	                    // Update range if both dates are valid
339	                    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (&ctx.parsed_start, &ctx.parsed_end) {
340	                        ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(start.clone(), end.clone())));
341	                    }
342	                }))
343	            }
344	
345	            (_, Event::EndInputChange { value }) => {
346	                let text = value.clone();
347	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
348	                    ctx.parsed_end = parse_date(&text, &ctx.format, &ctx.locale);
349	                    ctx.end_input_text = text;
350	                    ctx.active_field = ActiveField::End;
351	                    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (&ctx.parsed_start, &ctx.parsed_end) {
352	                        ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(start.clone(), end.clone())));
353	                    }
354	                }))
355	            }
356	
357	            (State::Open, Event::KeyDown { key }) if *key == KeyboardKey::Escape => {
358	                Self::transition(state, &Event::Close, ctx, _props)
359	            }
360	
361	            (State::Closed, Event::KeyDown { key }) if *key == KeyboardKey::ArrowDown => {
362	                Self::transition(state, &Event::Open, ctx, _props)
363	            }
364	
365	            (_, Event::FocusOut) if *state == State::Open => {
366	                Self::transition(state, &Event::Close, ctx, _props)
367	            }
368	
369	            _ => None,
370	        }
371	    }
372	
373	    fn connect<'a>(
374	        state: &'a Self::State,
375	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
376	        props: &'a Self::Props,
377	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
378	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
379	        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
380	    }
381	}
382	```

567	    /// Attributes for the hidden input element (form submission).

570	    /// and `end_hidden_input_attrs()` instead for separate form fields.
571	    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
572	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
573	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();
574	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
575	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
576	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
577	        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {
578	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
579	        }
580	        // Submit range as two ISO 8601 dates separated by "/"
581	        if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
582	            let iso = format!("{}/{}", range.start.to_iso_string(), range.end.to_iso_string());
583	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso);
584	        }
585	        attrs
586	    }

622	            is_range: true,
623	            visible_months: 2,
624	            page_behavior: PageBehavior::Visible,

633	    /// Screen reader description of the full date range (e.g., "March 1 to March 15").
634	    /// Returns `None` if no complete range is selected.
635	    /// The adapter can use this as an `aria-description` on the root or as a live announcement.
636	    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {
637	        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;
638	        let start = format_date(&range.start, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);
639	        let end = format_date(&range.end, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);
640	        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(&start, &end, &self.ctx.locale))
641	    }

643	    // -- Imperative methods -----------------------------------------------

645	    /// Open the popover.

647	    /// Close the popover.

649	    /// Toggle the popover.

653	    /// Get the selected range.

```
=== date_field::Props fields ===
pub struct Props {
    /// The ID of the field.
    pub id: String,

    /// The externally controlled value of the field.
    ///
    /// `None` means the field is uncontrolled. `Some(None)` means the parent is
    /// controlling the field as empty. `Some(Some(date))` means the parent is
    /// controlling a concrete date.
    pub value: Option<Option<CalendarDate>>,

    /// The initial value used when the field is uncontrolled.
    pub default_value: Option<CalendarDate>,

    /// The calendar system used to validate and edit date values.
    pub calendar: CalendarSystem,

    /// The editable date granularity.
    pub granularity: DateGranularity,

    /// The minimum allowed date value.
    pub min_value: Option<CalendarDate>,

    /// The maximum allowed date value.
    pub max_value: Option<CalendarDate>,

    /// Whether the field is disabled.
    pub disabled: bool,

    /// Whether the field is read-only.
    pub readonly: bool,

    /// Whether the field is required for form submission.
    pub required: bool,

    /// Whether the field should receive focus on mount.
    pub auto_focus: bool,

    /// The visible label text of the field.
    pub label: String,

    /// The accessible label when no visible label is used.
    pub aria_label: Option<String>,

    /// The ID reference used to label the field group.
    pub aria_labelledby: Option<String>,

    /// Extra ID references used to describe the field group.
    pub aria_describedby: Option<String>,

    /// Optional descriptive help text for the field.
    pub description: Option<String>,

    /// Optional validation error text for the field.
    pub error_message: Option<String>,

    /// Whether the field is currently invalid.
    pub invalid: bool,

    /// Optional form field name for the hidden input.
    pub name: Option<String>,

    /// Optional override for rendered segment order.
    pub segment_order: Option<Vec<DateSegmentKind>>,

    /// Whether numeric segments should force fixed-width leading zeroes.
    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,
}
```

The spec's `DateFormat`, `format_date`/`parse_date` string model, and `calendar::Props { is_range }` are all drift from an older imagined API.     pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
```
        date_field::Props {
            id: self.ctx.ids.part("start"),
            value: Some(self.ctx.start_date.clone()),
            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),
            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),
            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
            required: self.ctx.required,
            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(
                self.ctx.start_date.as_ref(),
                self.ctx.min.as_ref(),
                self.ctx.max.as_ref(),
            ),
            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,
            ..date_field::Props::default()
        }
    }
```

The proven `date_range_field` uses `StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`/`EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)` events and real `date_field::Props` fields (`min_value`/`max_value`, no `format`). The spec's text-based model (`StartInputChange { value: String }`, `parse_date`, `start_input_text`, `DateFormat`) is drift — `date_field` is a segmented field emitting date values, not a text input. 708	
709	| Part           | Element       | Key Attributes                                                      |
710	| -------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
711	| `Root`         | `<div>`       | `data-ars-scope="date-range-picker"`, `data-ars-part="root"`, state |
712	| `Label`        | `<label>`     | `for="{id}-start-input"`                                            |
713	| `Control`      | `<div>`       | `role="group"`, `aria-labelledby`                                   |
714	| `StartInput`   | _(DateField)_ | Start date field                                                    |
715	| `Separator`    | `<span>`      | `aria-hidden="true"`                                                |
716	| `EndInput`     | _(DateField)_ | End date field                                                      |
717	| `Trigger`      | `<button>`    | `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`          |
718	| `ClearTrigger` | `<button>`    | `aria-label="Clear date range"`                                     |
719	| `Positioner`   | `<div>`       | Floating positioner (via positioning engine)                        |
720	| `Content`      | `<div>`       | `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="false"`, `aria-labelledby`            |
721	| `Description`  | `<div>`       | Help text, wired via `aria-describedby`                             |
722	| `ErrorMessage` | `<div>`       | `role="alert"`, validation error                                    |
723	| `HiddenInput`  | `<input>`     | `type="hidden"`, ISO 8601 range value                               |
724	
725	## 3. Accessibility
726	
727	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
728	
729	| Element        | Role/Attribute      | Details                                                                       |
730	| -------------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
731	| `Control`      | `role="group"`      | `aria-labelledby` points to `Label`                                           |
732	| `Trigger`      | `<button>`          | `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` points to Content  |
733	| `Content`      | `role="dialog"`     | `aria-modal="false"`, `aria-labelledby` points to Label                       |
734	| `ErrorMessage` | `role="alert"`      | Announced immediately on render                                               |
735	| `Root`         | --                  | `data-ars-invalid` when validation fails                                      |
736	| `StartInput`   | spinbutton segments | `aria-label` from `messages.start_label`; delegates to DateField ARIA pattern |
737	| `EndInput`     | spinbutton segments | `aria-label` from `messages.end_label`; delegates to DateField ARIA pattern   |
738	
739	### 3.2 Keyboard Interaction
740	
741	| Key         | Context         | Action                                        |
742	| ----------- | --------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
743	| `ArrowDown` | Trigger / Input | Open the popover                              |
744	| `Escape`    | Popover open    | Close the popover, restore focus to trigger   |
745	| `Tab`       | Within calendar | Close the popover                             |
746	| `Enter`     | Calendar cell   | Complete range selection (if second endpoint) |
747	| `Space`     | Calendar cell   | Complete range selection (if second endpoint) |
748	
749	### 3.3 Focus Flow
750	
751	- When the popover opens, focus moves into the Calendar grid.
752	- When a range is completed, the popover closes and focus returns to the trigger.
753	- Start and End inputs are linked via `aria-labelledby` to the shared label.
754	- Tab navigation flows: StartInput -> Separator (skipped) -> EndInput -> Trigger -> ClearTrigger.
755	- `aria-describedby` wires to Description and ErrorMessage parts when present.
756	
757	## 4. Internationalization
758	
759	### 4.1 Messages
760	
761	```rust
762	/// Messages for the DateRangePicker component.
763	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
764	pub struct Messages {
765	    /// Trigger button label (default: "Open date range picker").
766	    pub trigger_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
767	    /// Label for the start date field.
768	    pub start_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
769	    /// Label for the end date field.
770	    pub end_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
771	    /// Describes the selected range (e.g. "March 1 to March 15").
772	    pub range_description: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str, &str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
773	    /// Accessible label for the clear trigger button (default: "Clear date range").
774	    pub clear_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
775	}
776	
777	impl Default for Messages {
778	    fn default() -> Self {
779	        Self {
780	            trigger_label: MessageFn::static_str("Open date range picker"),
781	            start_label: MessageFn::static_str("Start date"),
782	            end_label: MessageFn::static_str("End date"),
783	            range_description: MessageFn::new(|start, end, _locale| format!("{} to {}", start, end)),
784	            clear_label: MessageFn::static_str("Clear date range"),
785	        }
786	    }
787	}
788	
789	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
790	```
791	
792	## 5. Form Integration
793	
794	- Hidden `<input>` submits the range as two ISO 8601 dates separated by `/` (e.g., `2024-01-10/2024-01-20`).
795	- `name` attribute set from Props.
796	- Reset restores `default_value`.
797	- Validation states reflected via `data-ars-invalid` on Root.
798	- `aria-describedby` wires to Description and ErrorMessage parts.
799	- `aria-required` set when `required` is true.
800	- Disabled/readonly propagation from form context per `07-forms.md` S15.
801	
802	## 6. Library Parity
803	
804	> Compared against: Ark UI (`DatePicker` with `selectionMode: 'range'`), React Aria (`DateRangePicker`).
805	
806	### 6.1 Props
807	
808	| Feature               | ars-ui            | Ark UI                | React Aria                  | Notes                                               |
809	| --------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------- | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
810	| Controlled range      | `value`           | `value` (DateValue[]) | `value` (RangeValue)        | Equivalent                                          |
811	| Default range         | `default_value`   | `defaultValue`        | `defaultValue`              | Equivalent                                          |
812	| Min/max               | `min`, `max`      | `min`, `max`          | `minValue`, `maxValue`      | Equivalent                                          |
813	| Disabled              | `disabled`        | `disabled`            | `isDisabled`                | Equivalent                                          |
814	| Read-only             | `readonly`        | `readOnly`            | `isReadOnly`                | Equivalent                                          |
815	| Required              | `required`        | `required`            | `isRequired`                | Equivalent                                          |
816	| Close on select       | `close_on_select` | `closeOnSelect`       | `shouldCloseOnSelect`       | Equivalent                                          |
817	| Locale                | `locale`          | `locale`              | -- (context)                | Equivalent                                          |
818	| Format                | `format`          | `format`              | --                          | Equivalent                                          |
819	| Name                  | `name`            | `name`                | `startName`, `endName`      | React Aria has separate form names                  |
820	| Positioning           | `positioning`     | `positioning`         | --                          | Equivalent                                          |
821	| Allows non-contiguous | --                | --                    | `allowsNonContiguousRanges` | React Aria feature                                  |
822	| Max visible months    | --                | `numOfMonths`         | `maxVisibleMonths`          | ars-ui uses `visible_months: 2` default on Calendar |
823	| Start/end form names  | --                | --                    | `startName`, `endName`      | React Aria has separate form names for start/end    |
824	
825	**Gaps:**
826	
827	- `allowsNonContiguousRanges`: React Aria allows selecting ranges that span unavailable dates. Currently ars-ui's RangeCalendar blocks selection of unavailable dates. This is a niche feature; not adopting.
828	
829	### 6.2 Anatomy
830	
831	| Part          | ars-ui         | Ark UI            | React Aria                 | Notes                               |
832	| ------------- | -------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------- | ----------------------------------- |
833	| Root          | `Root`         | `Root`            | `DateRangePicker`          | Equivalent                          |
834	| Label         | `Label`        | `Label`           | `Label`                    | Equivalent                          |
835	| Control       | `Control`      | `Control`         | `Group`                    | Equivalent                          |
836	| Start input   | `StartInput`   | `Input` (index=0) | `DateInput` (slot="start") | Equivalent                          |
837	| Separator     | `Separator`    | --                | --                         | ars-ui adds a visual separator      |
838	| End input     | `EndInput`     | `Input` (index=1) | `DateInput` (slot="end")   | Equivalent                          |
839	| Trigger       | `Trigger`      | `Trigger`         | `Button`                   | Equivalent                          |
840	| Clear trigger | `ClearTrigger` | `ClearTrigger`    | --                         | ars-ui and Ark UI have clear button |
841	| Positioner    | `Positioner`   | `Positioner`      | --                         | Equivalent                          |
842	| Content       | `Content`      | `Content`         | `Popover` + `Dialog`       | Equivalent                          |
843	| Description   | `Description`  | --                | `Text` (slot)              | Equivalent                          |
844	| Error message | `ErrorMessage` | --                | `FieldError`               | Equivalent                          |
845	| Hidden input  | `HiddenInput`  | --                | --                         | ars-ui form integration             |
846	
847	**Gaps:** None worth adopting.
848	
849	### 6.3 Events
850	
851	| Callback     | ars-ui                 | Ark UI          | React Aria      | Notes      |
852	| ------------ | ---------------------- | --------------- | --------------- | ---------- |
853	| Range change | `SelectRangeComplete`  | `onValueChange` | `onChange`      | Equivalent |
854	| Open change  | `Open` / `Close`       | `onOpenChange`  | `onOpenChange`  | Equivalent |
855	| Focus change | `FocusIn` / `FocusOut` | `onFocusChange` | `onFocusChange` | Equivalent |
856	
857	**Gaps:** None.
858	
859	### 6.4 Features
860	
861	| Feature               | ars-ui                      | Ark UI            | React Aria            |
862	| --------------------- | --------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------- |
863	| Two-field input       | Yes (StartInput + EndInput) | Yes (Input index) | Yes (start/end slots) |
864	| Calendar popover      | Yes                         | Yes               | Yes                   |
865	| Range normalization   | Yes                         | Yes               | Yes                   |
866	| Close on select       | Yes                         | Yes               | Yes                   |
867	| Clear button          | Yes                         | Yes               | No                    |
868	| Multi-month calendar  | Yes (default 2)             | Yes               | Yes                   |
869	| Non-contiguous ranges | No                          | No                | Yes                   |
870	| Separate form names   | No                          | No                | Yes                   |
871	| Hidden form input     | Yes                         | No                | No                    |
872	
873	**Gaps:** None.
874	
875	### 6.5 Summary
876	
877	- **Overall:** Full parity.
878	- **Divergences:** React Aria supports `allowsNonContiguousRanges` which is a niche feature not adopted. ars-ui submits ranges via a single hidden input while React Aria uses separate form names.
879	- **Recommended additions:** None.
880

```
=== range_calendar::Props derive + Default impl ===
136:pub struct Props {
189:impl Default for Props {
--- range_calendar Props fields ---
pub struct Props {
    /// Component instance ID.
    pub id: String,

    /// Controlled range value. `None` means uncontrolled.
    pub value: Option<Option<DateRange>>,

    /// Default range value for uncontrolled mode.
    pub default_value: Option<DateRange>,

    /// Minimum selectable date.
    pub min: Option<CalendarDate>,

    /// Maximum selectable date.
    pub max: Option<CalendarDate>,

    /// Whether the entire calendar is non-interactive.
    pub disabled: bool,

    /// Whether navigation and focus are allowed but selection is blocked.
    pub readonly: bool,

    /// Predicate returning `true` for dates that are unavailable.
    pub is_date_unavailable: Option<IsDateUnavailableFn>,

    /// Explicit override of the locale default first day of week.
    pub first_day_of_week: Option<Weekday>,

    /// Whether the head row exposes ISO week numbers.
    pub show_week_numbers: bool,

    /// Right-to-left layout direction.
    pub is_rtl: bool,

=== date_range_field: range_description method (how it formats dates) ===
    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;

        let start = format_date_label(
            &range.start,
            self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(),
            &self.ctx.locale,
        );

        let end = format_date_label(&range.end, self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(), &self.ctx.locale);

        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(
            &start,
            &end,
            &self.ctx.locale,
        ))
    }
```

> AGENT

I now have the complete picture. `range_calendar::Props` impls `Default` (so I can spread it), and `date_range_field` formats dates via an `intl_backend` for `range_description`. 

Here's the situation: **the DateRangePicker spec has substantial drift from the real dependency APIs**, because it was written before `date_field` and `range_calendar` were finalized. The spec models the inputs as text fields with a `format: DateFormat` string and parses raw strings, and composes `calendar { is_range: true }`. In reality:

- `date_field` is a **segmented** field (emits `Option<CalendarDate>`, has no `format` string — uses `calendar`/`granularity`/`segment_order`); `DateFormat` doesn't exist.
- Range selection lives in the dedicated **`range_calendar`** component (dependency #287); `calendar::Props` has no `is_range`/`range_start`/`range_end`.
- `CalendarDate::to_iso_string()` doesn't exist (it's `to_iso8601()`); `DateRange::normalized()` returns `Option`, so the spec's `set(Some(normalized(...)))` is a type error.
- `range_calendar` requires a `today` date; the spec has no `today` prop. `PositioningOptions` exists but DatePicker (the analog) omits it (positioning is an adapter concern per the issue's element-handling note).

The proven sibling `date_range_field` already establishes the correct value-based pattern (`StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`, `recompute_range` via `DateRange::normalized`). Let me confirm two decisions before writing the plan.

```
=== date_range_field init: how intl_backend pulled from env ===
    fn init(
        props: &Self::Props,
        env: &Env,
        messages: &Self::Messages,
    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
        let value = if let Some(value) = &props.value {
            Bindable::controlled(value.clone())
        } else {
            Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone())
        };

        let initial_range = value.get().clone();
        let start_date = initial_range.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());
        let end_date = initial_range.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());

        let ctx = Context {
            value,
            start_date,
            end_date,
            active_field: None,
            min: props.min.clone(),
            max: props.max.clone(),
            locale: env.locale.clone(),
            intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),
            messages: messages.clone(),
            disabled: props.disabled,
            readonly: props.readonly,
            required: props.required,
            name: props.name.clone(),
            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),
            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),
            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
            has_description: props.has_description,
            has_error_message: props.has_error_message,
            force_leading_zeros: props.force_leading_zeros,
        };

        (State::Idle, ctx)
    }

    fn transition(
        _state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        ctx: &Self::Context,
        _props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
        // `SyncProps` must process even when disabled so adapter-driven prop
        // updates (including re-enabling) still reach the context.
        if ctx.disabled && !matches!(event, Event::SyncProps(_)) {
            return None;
        }

        match event {
            Event::SyncProps(props) => {
                let props = props.as_ref().clone();
                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
                    sync_props(ctx, &props);
                }))
            }
```

```
//! Spec-conformance tests for ars-components.
//!
//! Each module asserts that a component's `Part` enum matches the
//! anatomy table declared in `spec/components/.../{component}.md` §3.
//! The test layer is intentionally tiny — it leans on the derive-generated
//! `Part::scope()`, `Part::all()`, and `Part::name()` to compare against a
//! hand-rolled allow-list of `(Part, expected-kebab-name)` pairs per
//! component.
//!
//! New components SHOULD add a sibling module here (or extend an existing
//! one) so any drift between the spec's declared parts and the impl is
//! caught at build time, not by a reviewer's eye.
//!
//! See `spec/foundation/10-component-spec-template.md` "Spec-Conformance
//! Test (all tiers)" for the workspace convention.
```
