{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.claude/plans/implement-ta[REDACTED_SK].md",
  "content": "# Task #290 — Implement DateRangePicker agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nGitHub issue #290 (epic #224, 5 pts) asks for the framework-agnostic core of **DateRangePicker**:\na popover composing **two DateField inputs** + a **RangeCalendar** grid, with bidirectional\nrange coordination, validation, form integration, and ARIA/data attrs. Adapter components\n(Leptos/Dioxus) and all live DOM concerns (focus, positioning, scroll, return-focus) are out of\nscope. Dependencies #284 (DateRangeField), #287 (RangeCalendar), #289 (DatePicker) are all merged.\n\n### Spec drift (resolved in this PR per the spec-synchronization rule)\n\nThe spec (`spec/components/date-time/date-range-picker.md`) predates the finalized dependency\nAPIs and will not compile as written. The user approved **amending the spec to match the real\nAPIs** (value-based fields) and **adding a preset API**. Confirmed-against-code corrections:\n\n| Spec as written | Reality | Resolution |\n|---|---|---|\n| `format: DateFormat`, `format_date`/`parse_date`, `start_input_text`/`parsed_start` | `DateFormat` does not exist; `date_field` is segmented and emits `Option<CalendarDate>` (fields: `value`, `min_value`, `max_value`, `aria_label`, `force_leading_zeros`; no `format`) | Drop `format`/text model. Store `start_date`/`end_date: Option<CalendarDate>`. Events `StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)` / `EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)` (mirror `date_range_field`) |\n| `calendar::Props { is_range: true, range_start, range_end }` | `calendar::Props` has no `is_range`; range lives in the dedicated **`range_calendar`** component (#287) | `range_calendar_props() -> range_calendar::Props` |\n| `CalendarDate::to_iso_string()` | Method is `to_iso8601()`; `DateRange::to_iso8601()` yields `\"start/end\"` | Use `range.to_iso8601()` for combined hidden input; `range.start.to_iso8601()` for split |\n| `ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(a,b)))` | `normalized()` returns `Option<DateRange>` (type error) | `match DateRange::normalized(a,b) { Some(r)=>set(Some(r)), None=>set(None) }` (mirror `date_range_field::recompute_range`) |\n| No `today` prop | `range_calendar::Props.today` is required | Add `today: CalendarDate` prop, forward to `range_calendar_props()` (mirror `date_picker`) |\n| `positioning: PositioningOptions` | Type exists but DatePicker omits it; positioning is an adapter concern per the issue's element-handling note | Drop from agnostic Props |\n| Machine omits `type Effect`, no `SyncProps`, no `Clear` event | `Machine::Effect` is non-defaulted; controlled `value` + `ClearTrigger` part need wiring | Add `type Effect = NoEffect`, `Event::SyncProps(Box<Props>)` + `on_props_changed`, `Event::Clear` |\n\n## Design\n\nDateRangePicker owns the canonical range and popover state; it does **not** run child machines.\nLike DatePicker, it exposes `*_props()` builders the adapter feeds to child machines and accepts\nbridged events. The two-field value logic mirrors the proven `date_range_field`; the popover\nopen/close mirrors `date_picker`.\n\n**State** (unchanged): `Closed`, `Open`.\n\n**Events** (amended): `Open`, `Close`, `Toggle`, `SelectRangeComplete { range: DateRange }`,\n`StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`, `EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`,\n`SelectPreset { index: usize }`, `Clear`, `FocusIn`, `FocusOut`, `KeyDown { key: KeyboardKey }`,\n`SyncProps(Box<Props>)`.\n\n**Context** (amended): `value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>`, `open: Bindable<bool>`,\n`start_date`/`end_date: Option<CalendarDate>`, `active_field: ActiveField`,\n`min`/`max: Option<CalendarDate>`, `today: CalendarDate`, `presets: Vec<Preset>`,\n`visible_months: usize`, `is_rtl: bool`, `locale: Locale`, `intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>`\n(for `range_description` formatting), `messages: Messages`, `disabled`/`readonly`/`required`,\n`force_leading_zeros: bool`, `has_description`/`has_error_message: bool`,\n`name`/`start_name`/`end_name: Option<String>`, `ids: ComponentIds`.\n\n**Props** (amended): `id`, `value: Option<Option<DateRange>>`, `default_value`, `min`, `max`,\n`today` (default `CalendarDate::new_gregorian(2025,1,1)` like DatePicker), `presets: Vec<Preset>`,\n`visible_months: usize` (default `2`), `is_rtl`, `disabled`, `readonly`, `required`,\n`force_leading_zeros`, `has_description`, `has_error_message`, `name`, `start_name`, `end_name`,\n`close_on_select: bool` (default `true`). Derives `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default, HasId`.\n\n**New public `Preset` type** (deterministic, no closures — consumer supplies a pre-localized\nlabel + a concrete range computed relative to the same injected `today`):\n```rust\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Preset {\n    pub label: String,\n    pub range: DateRange,\n}\n```\n\n**Anatomy `Part`** (amended — adds data-carrying `PresetTrigger`, mirroring\n`range_calendar::Part::Row { week_index }`):\n`Root, Label, Control, StartInput, Separator, EndInput, Trigger, ClearTrigger,\nPresetTrigger { index: usize }, Positioner, Content, Description, ErrorMessage, HiddenInput`.\n\n**Transition logic:**\n- `disabled` guard returns `None` for all events except `SyncProps` (mirror `date_range_field`).\n- `Open`/`Toggle` (Closed) → Open; `Close`/`Toggle` (Open) → Closed.\n- `SelectRangeComplete { range }`: set `start_date`/`end_date`/`value`; close iff `props.close_on_select`.\n- `SelectPreset { index }`: look up `ctx.presets[index]`, apply its range like `SelectRangeComplete`.\n- `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`: update the field, set `active_field`,\n  recompute range via `DateRange::normalized` (None when incomplete/incomparable). Blocked when `readonly`.\n- `Clear`: clear `value`/`start_date`/`end_date`.\n- `KeyDown` Escape (Open) → Close; ArrowDown (Closed) → Open.\n- `FocusOut` (Open) → Close. `FocusIn` → `None` (kept for adapter-symmetry/spec parity).\n- `SyncProps`: re-apply controlled `value` via `Bindable::sync_controlled`; refresh cached\n  `min`/`max`/`today`/`presets`/flags (mirror `date_range_field::sync_props`). `open` is always uncontrolled.\n\n**Api** (amended, no live-DOM ops): `root_attrs` (incl. `data-ars-invalid` when range out of\nbounds — reuse `date_range_field`'s `date_out_of_bounds` check), `label_attrs` (`for={id}-start`),\n`control_attrs` (`role=group`), `separator_attrs` (`aria-hidden`),\n`start_field_props()`/`end_field_props() -> date_field::Props` (real fields: `value`, `min_value`,\n`max_value` bounded by the sibling field, `aria_label`, `force_leading_zeros`, `invalid`),\n`trigger_attrs`, `clear_trigger_attrs` (disabled when no value/disabled),\n`preset_trigger_attrs(index)` + `presets()` + `preset_label(index)`,\n`positioner_attrs`, `content_attrs` (`role=dialog`, `aria-modal=false`, `aria-labelledby`),\n`description_attrs`, `error_message_attrs` (`role=alert`),\n`hidden_input_attrs` (`range.to_iso8601()`), `start_hidden_input_attrs`/`end_hidden_input_attrs`,\n`range_calendar_props() -> range_calendar::Props` (`..Default::default()` + value/min/max/today/\nvisible_months/is_rtl), `range_description() -> Option<String>` (via shared `format_date_label`),\nimperative `open/close/toggle/clear/is_open/selected_range`, and `on_*` dispatch helpers.\n`ConnectApi::part_attrs` dispatches every `Part`.\n\n**Messages** (per spec §4.1, unchanged): `trigger_label`, `start_label`, `end_label`,\n`range_description: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str,&str,&Locale)->String + Send + Sync>`, `clear_label`.\nAdd `preset` labels come from `Preset.label`, not Messages.\n\n## Files\n\nCreate:\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_picker/mod.rs` — machine, `State`, `Event`,\n  `ActiveField`, `Context`, `Props`, `Preset`, `Part`, `Api`, `Messages`. Directory-backed module.\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_picker/tests.rs` — unit + snapshot tests.\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_picker/snapshots/*.snap` — committed insta fixtures.\n\nModify:\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs` — add `pub mod date_range_picker;`; promote the\n  duplicated private `format_date_label(date, &dyn IntlBackend, &Locale)` to a `pub(crate)` shared\n  helper here and have `date_range_field`, `range_calendar`, and the new module call it (dedup;\n  identical signature already exists in both siblings).\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs` and `.../range_calendar/mod.rs` —\n  use the shared `format_date_label` (remove their local copies).\n- `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs` — add\n  `date_range_picker_anatomy_matches_spec` listing all 14 parts (use a default index for\n  `PresetTrigger { index: 0 }`, mirroring how `Row`/`Cell` are listed).\n- `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs` — add a `date_range_picker`\n  arbitrary-event strategy + invariant block (mirror the existing `range_calendar`/`date_range_field` entries).\n- `spec/components/date-time/date-range-picker.md` — rewrite §1.2 Events, §1.3 Context, §1.4 Props,\n  §1.8 Machine, §1.9 Connect/API, §2 Anatomy (add `PresetTrigger`), and update §1 intro/§6 parity to\n  match the value-based composition + the new `Preset` API; fix all the drift rows in the table above.\n\nNo `lib.rs`, `manifest.toml`, or catalog changes (component already specced/registered;\naccessed via `ars_components::date_time::date_range_picker`).\n\n## Workflow\n\n1. Move issue #290 to **In Progress** on the GitHub Project board.\n2. **TDD:** write the named tests first (unit + ARIA + snapshot) referencing the amended API, watch\n   them fail, then implement `mod.rs` to green. Amend the spec alongside the implementation so §1/§2\n   stay the contract.\n3. Reuse, do not reinvent: `DateRange`/`CalendarDate`/`IntlBackend` from `ars_i18n`;\n   `date_field::Props` (segmented fields); `range_calendar::Props`; `Bindable`,\n   `ComponentIds`, `MessageFn`, `NoEffect`, `TransitionPlan`, `ConnectApi` from `ars_core`;\n   `date_range_field`'s `recompute_range`/`date_out_of_bounds`/`sync_props` patterns;\n   the shared `format_date_label`.\n4. Zero warnings, `missing_docs` satisfied on every public item.\n5. **MANDATORY** `post-implementation-audit` skill (all three phases; every finding fixed in this PR).\n6. Present for review before any commit.\n7. After approval: `cargo xci-fast`; fix failures; commit + push PR to `main` with `Closes #290`.\n8. `select_range_complete_stays_open_when_not_close_on_select`;\n`start_value_change_recomputes_range`; `end_value_change_recomputes_range`;\n`field_edit_syncs_into_range` (value sync: editing a field updates the canonical range/calendar highlight);\n`range_normalizes_when_start_after_end`; `out_of_bounds_marks_invalid`;\n`select_preset_applies_range_and_closes`; `clear_resets_value`; `escape_closes`; `arrow_down_opens`;\n`focusout_closes`; `disabled_ignores_events`; `readonly_blocks_selection_allows_open`;\n`controlled_value_syncs_via_set_props`.\n\nARIA/Api: `trigger_haspopup_expanded_controls`; `clear_trigger_disabled_when_empty`;\n`content_dialog_attrs`; `error_message_alert_role`; `hidden_input_iso_range` (two hidden inputs:\ncombined `start/end` + split `start_name`/`end_name`); `start_field_props_bounds_and_label`;\n`end_field_props_bounds_and_label`; `range_calendar_props_forwards_today_and_visible_months`;\n`preset_trigger_attrs`; `range_description_when_complete`.\n\nSnapshot (`assert_snapshot!`, `cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`): every anatomy part and each\noutput-affecting branch — root (closed/open/disabled/readonly/required/invalid), label, control,\nseparator, trigger (closed/open/disabled), clear-trigger (empty/with-value), preset-trigger,\npositioner, content, description, error-message, hidden-input (empty/with-range), split start/end\nhidden inputs, and `start_field_props`/`end_field_props` rendered as `AttrMap`.\n\nProptest: DateRangePicker added to `proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs` (no panics; ## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Task #290 — Implement DateRangePicker agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #290 (epic #224, 5 pts) asks for the framework-agnostic core of **DateRangePicker**:
a popover composing **two DateField inputs** + a **RangeCalendar** grid, with bidirectional
range coordination, validation, form integration, and ARIA/data attrs. Adapter components
(Leptos/Dioxus) and all live DOM concerns (focus, positioning, scroll, return-focus) are out of
scope. Dependencies #284 (DateRangeField), #287 (RangeCalendar), #289 (DatePicker) are all merged.

### Spec drift (resolved in this PR per the spec-synchronization rule)

The spec (`spec/components/date-time/date-range-picker.md`) predates the finalized dependency
APIs and will not compile as written. The user approved **amending the spec to match the real
APIs** (value-based fields) and **adding a preset API**. Confirmed-against-code corrections:

| Spec as written | Reality | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| `format: DateFormat`, `format_date`/`parse_date`, `start_input_text`/`parsed_start` | `DateFormat` does not exist; `date_field` is segmented and emits `Option<CalendarDate>` (fields: `value`, `min_value`, `max_value`, `aria_label`, `force_leading_zeros`; no `format`) | Drop `format`/text model. Store `start_date`/`end_date: Option<CalendarDate>`. Events `StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)` / `EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)` (mirror `date_range_field`) |
| `calendar::Props { is_range: true, range_start, range_end }` | `calendar::Props` has no `is_range`; range lives in the dedicated **`range_calendar`** component (#287) | `range_calendar_props() -> range_calendar::Props` |
| `CalendarDate::to_iso_string()` | Method is `to_iso8601()`; `DateRange::to_iso8601()` yields `"start/end"` | Use `range.to_iso8601()` for combined hidden input; `range.start.to_iso8601()` for split |
| `ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(a,b)))` | `normalized()` returns `Option<DateRange>` (type error) | `match DateRange::normalized(a,b) { Some(r)=>set(Some(r)), None=>set(None) }` (mirror `date_range_field::recompute_range`) |
| No `today` prop | `range_calendar::Props.today` is required | Add `today: CalendarDate` prop, forward to `range_calendar_props()` (mirror `date_picker`) |
| `positioning: PositioningOptions` | Type exists but DatePicker omits it; positioning is an adapter concern per the issue's element-handling note | Drop from agnostic Props |
| Machine omits `type Effect`, no `SyncProps`, no `Clear` event | `Machine::Effect` is non-defaulted; controlled `value` + `ClearTrigger` part need wiring | Add `type Effect = NoEffect`, `Event::SyncProps(Box<Props>)` + `on_props_changed`, `Event::Clear` |

## Design

DateRangePicker owns the canonical range and popover state; it does **not** run child machines.
Like DatePicker, it exposes `*_props()` builders the adapter feeds to child machines and accepts
bridged events. The two-field value logic mirrors the proven `date_range_field`; the popover
open/close mirrors `date_picker`.

**State** (unchanged): `Closed`, `Open`.

**Events** (amended): `Open`, `Close`, `Toggle`, `SelectRangeComplete { range: DateRange }`,
`StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`, `EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`,
`SelectPreset { index: usize }`, `Clear`, `FocusIn`, `FocusOut`, `KeyDown { key: KeyboardKey }`,
`SyncProps(Box<Props>)`.

**Context** (amended): `value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>`, `open: Bindable<bool>`,
`start_date`/`end_date: Option<CalendarDate>`, `active_field: ActiveField`,
`min`/`max: Option<CalendarDate>`, `today: CalendarDate`, `presets: Vec<Preset>`,
`visible_months: usize`, `is_rtl: bool`, `locale: Locale`, `intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>`
(for `range_description` formatting), `messages: Messages`, `disabled`/`readonly`/`required`,
`force_leading_zeros: bool`, `has_description`/`has_error_message: bool`,
`name`/`start_name`/`end_name: Option<String>`, `ids: ComponentIds`.

**Props** (amended): `id`, `value: Option<Option<DateRange>>`, `default_value`, `min`, `max`,
`today` (default `CalendarDate::new_gregorian(2025,1,1)` like DatePicker), `presets: Vec<Preset>`,
`visible_months: usize` (default `2`), `is_rtl`, `disabled`, `readonly`, `required`,
`force_leading_zeros`, `has_description`, `has_error_message`, `name`, `start_name`, `end_name`,
`close_on_select: bool` (default `true`). Derives `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default, HasId`.

**New public `Preset` type** (deterministic, no closures — consumer supplies a pre-localized
label + a concrete range computed relative to the same injected `today`):
```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Preset {
    pub label: String,
    pub range: DateRange,
}
```

**Anatomy `Part`** (amended — adds data-carrying `PresetTrigger`, mirroring
`range_calendar::Part::Row { week_index }`):
`Root, Label, Control, StartInput, Separator, EndInput, Trigger, ClearTrigger,
PresetTrigger { index: usize }, Positioner, Content, Description, ErrorMessage, HiddenInput`.

**Transition logic:**
- `disabled` guard returns `None` for all events except `SyncProps` (mirror `date_range_field`).
- `Open`/`Toggle` (Closed) → Open; `Close`/`Toggle` (Open) → Closed.
- `SelectRangeComplete { range }`: set `start_date`/`end_date`/`value`; close iff `props.close_on_select`.
- `SelectPreset { index }`: look up `ctx.presets[index]`, apply its range like `SelectRangeComplete`.
- `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`: update the field, set `active_field`,
  recompute range via `DateRange::normalized` (None when incomplete/incomparable). Blocked when `readonly`.
- `Clear`: clear `value`/`start_date`/`end_date`.
- `KeyDown` Escape (Open) → Close; ArrowDown (Closed) → Open.
- `FocusOut` (Open) → Close. `FocusIn` → `None` (kept for adapter-symmetry/spec parity).
- `SyncProps`: re-apply controlled `value` via `Bindable::sync_controlled`; refresh cached
  `min`/`max`/`today`/`presets`/flags (mirror `date_range_field::sync_props`). `open` is always uncontrolled.

**Api** (amended, no live-DOM ops): `root_attrs` (incl. `data-ars-invalid` when range out of
bounds — reuse `date_range_field`'s `date_out_of_bounds` check), `label_attrs` (`for={id}-start`),
`control_attrs` (`role=group`), `separator_attrs` (`aria-hidden`),
`start_field_props()`/`end_field_props() -> date_field::Props` (real fields: `value`, `min_value`,
`max_value` bounded by the sibling field, `aria_label`, `force_leading_zeros`, `invalid`),
`trigger_attrs`, `clear_trigger_attrs` (disabled when no value/disabled),
`preset_trigger_attrs(index)` + `presets()` + `preset_label(index)`,
`positioner_attrs`, `content_attrs` (`role=dialog`, `aria-modal=false`, `aria-labelledby`),
`description_attrs`, `error_message_attrs` (`role=alert`),
`hidden_input_attrs` (`range.to_iso8601()`), `start_hidden_input_attrs`/`end_hidden_input_attrs`,
`range_calendar_props() -> range_calendar::Props` (`..Default::default()` + value/min/max/today/
visible_months/is_rtl), `range_description() -> Option<String>` (via shared `format_date_label`),
imperative `open/close/toggle/clear/is_open/selected_range`, and `on_*` dispatch helpers.
`ConnectApi::part_attrs` dispatches every `Part`.

**Messages** (per spec §4.1, unchanged): `trigger_label`, `start_label`, `end_label`,
`range_description: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str,&str,&Locale)->String + Send + Sync>`, `clear_label`.
Add `preset` labels come from `Preset.label`, not Messages.

## Files

Create:
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_picker/mod.rs` — machine, `State`, `Event`,
  `ActiveField`, `Context`, `Props`, `Preset`, `Part`, `Api`, `Messages`. Directory-backed module.
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_picker/tests.rs` — unit + snapshot tests.
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_picker/snapshots/*.snap` — committed insta fixtures.

Modify:
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs` — add `pub mod date_range_picker;`; promote the
  duplicated private `format_date_label(date, &dyn IntlBackend, &Locale)` to a `pub(crate)` shared
  helper here and have `date_range_field`, `range_calendar`, and the new module call it (dedup;
  identical signature already exists in both siblings).
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs` and `.../range_calendar/mod.rs` —
  use the shared `format_date_label` (remove their local copies).
- `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs` — add
  `date_range_picker_anatomy_matches_spec` listing all 14 parts (use a default index for
  `PresetTrigger { index: 0 }`, mirroring how `Row`/`Cell` are listed).
- `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs` — add a `date_range_picker`
  arbitrary-event strategy + invariant block (mirror the existing `range_calendar`/`date_range_field` entries).
- `spec/components/date-time/date-range-picker.md` — rewrite §1.2 Events, §1.3 Context, §1.4 Props,
  §1.8 Machine, §1.9 Connect/API, §2 Anatomy (add `PresetTrigger`), and update §1 intro/§6 parity to
  match the value-based composition + the new `Preset` API; fix all the drift rows in the table above.

No `lib.rs`, `manifest.toml`, or catalog changes (component already specced/registered;
accessed via `ars_components::date_time::date_range_picker`).

## Workflow

1. Move issue #290 to **In Progress** on the GitHub Project board.
2. **TDD:** write the named tests first (unit + ARIA + snapshot) referencing the amended API, watch
   them fail, then implement `mod.rs` to green. Amend the spec alongside the implementation so §1/§2
   stay the contract.
3. Reuse, do not reinvent: `DateRange`/`CalendarDate`/`IntlBackend` from `ars_i18n`;
   `date_field::Props` (segmented fields); `range_calendar::Props`; `Bindable`,
   `ComponentIds`, `MessageFn`, `NoEffect`, `TransitionPlan`, `ConnectApi` from `ars_core`;
   `date_range_field`'s `recompute_range`/`date_out_of_bounds`/`sync_props` patterns;
   the shared `format_date_label`.
4. Zero warnings, `missing_docs` satisfied on every public item.
5. **MANDATORY** `post-implementation-audit` skill (all three phases; every finding fixed in this PR).
6. Present for review before any commit.
7. After approval: `cargo xci-fast`; fix failures; commit + push PR to `main` with `Closes #290`.
8. Add `snapshot-reviewed` label (this PR creates `.snap` fixtures).
9. **MANDATORY** `waiting-for-codex-review` skill after first push and after each subsequent push.
10. Merge only after green CI + Codex 👍; then close #290.

## Tests to add (named, first)

Unit (transition): `initial_state_is_closed`; `open_and_close`; `toggle`;
`select_range_complete_sets_value_and_closes`; `select_range_complete_stays_open_when_not_close_on_select`;
`start_value_change_recomputes_range`; `end_value_change_recomputes_range`;
`field_edit_syncs_into_range` (value sync: editing a field updates the canonical range/calendar highlight);
`range_normalizes_when_start_after_end`; `out_of_bounds_marks_invalid`;
`select_preset_applies_range_and_closes`; `clear_resets_value`; `escape_closes`; `arrow_down_opens`;
`focusout_closes`; `disabled_ignores_events`; `readonly_blocks_selection_allows_open`;
`controlled_value_syncs_via_set_props`.

ARIA/Api: `trigger_haspopup_expanded_controls`; `clear_trigger_disabled_when_empty`;
`content_dialog_attrs`; `error_message_alert_role`; `hidden_input_iso_range` (two hidden inputs:
combined `start/end` + split `start_name`/`end_name`); `start_field_props_bounds_and_label`;
`end_field_props_bounds_and_label`; `range_calendar_props_forwards_today_and_visible_months`;
`preset_trigger_attrs`; `range_description_when_complete`.

Snapshot (`assert_snapshot!`, `cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`): every anatomy part and each
output-affecting branch — root (closed/open/disabled/readonly/required/invalid), label, control,
separator, trigger (closed/open/disabled), clear-trigger (empty/with-value), preset-trigger,
positioner, content, description, error-message, hidden-input (empty/with-range), split start/end
hidden inputs, and `start_field_props`/`end_field_props` rendered as `AttrMap`.

Proptest: DateRangePicker added to `proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs` (no panics; `value`
stays normalized; `open`↔state consistency).

Spec-conformance: `date_range_picker_anatomy_matches_spec` (14 parts, kebab names).

## Verification

- `cargo test -p ars-components date_range_picker` — all named unit/ARIA/snapshot tests pass, zero warnings.
- `cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject` — snapshots committed and referenced.
- `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance` and `--test proptest_state_machines`.
- `cargo xtask spec validate` — spec frontmatter still consistent after the amendment.
- `cargo llvm-cov test -p ars-components --text -- date_range_picker` — confirm new code meets the
  crate coverage threshold (no uncovered branches beyond no-op test closures).
- Final gate before push: `cargo xci-fast`.

1	//! DateRangeField component state machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! This module implements the framework-agnostic two-field date range input
4	//! defined in `spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md`. It composes two
5	//! [`date_field`] instances (start and end) with shared range coordination,
6	//! min/max validation, focus-field tracking, and form integration. There is no
7	//! popover — the two fields render inline with a visual separator between them.
8	
9	#[cfg(test)]
10	mod tests;

175	
176	impl Context {
177	    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the configured min/max
178	    /// bounds.
179	    ///
180	    /// The stored range always satisfies `start <= end` (it is normalized on
181	    /// every value change), so the only remaining source of invalidity is a
182	    /// start earlier than `min` or an end later than `max`.
183	    #[must_use]
184	    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {
185	        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else {
186	            return false;
187	        };
188	
189	        // A normalized range (`start <= end`) lies within `[min, max]` iff
190	        // neither endpoint is out of bounds.
191	        date_out_of_bounds(Some(&range.start), self.min.as_ref(), self.max.as_ref())
192	            || date_out_of_bounds(Some(&range.end), self.min.as_ref(), self.max.as_ref())
193	    }
194	}
195	
196	/// Returns `true` when `date` is present and falls outside the inclusive
197	/// `[min, max]` bounds (either bound absent means unbounded on that side).
198	fn date_out_of_bounds(
199	    date: Option<&CalendarDate>,
200	    min: Option<&CalendarDate>,
201	    max: Option<&CalendarDate>,
202	) -> bool {
203	    let Some(date) = date else {
204	        return false;
205	    };
206	
207	    let below_min =
208	        min.is_some_and(|min| matches!(date.compare_within_calendar(min), Some(Ordering::Less)));
209	    let above_max =
210	        max.is_some_and(|max| matches!(date.compare_within_calendar(max), Some(Ordering::Greater)));
211	
212	    below_min || above_max
213	}

413	/// State machine for the `DateRangeField` component.
414	#[derive(Debug)]
415	pub struct Machine;
416	
417	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
418	    type State = State;
419	    type Event = Event;
420	    type Context = Context;
421	    type Props = Props;
422	    type Messages = Messages;
423	    type Effect = NoEffect;
424	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
425	
426	    fn init(
427	        props: &Self::Props,
428	        env: &Env,
429	        messages: &Self::Messages,
430	    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
431	        let value = if let Some(value) = &props.value {
432	            Bindable::controlled(value.clone())
433	        } else {
434	            Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone())
435	        };
436	
437	        let initial_range = value.get().clone();
438	        let start_date = initial_range.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());
439	        let end_date = initial_range.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());
440	
441	        let ctx = Context {
442	            value,
443	            start_date,
444	            end_date,
445	            active_field: None,
446	            min: props.min.clone(),
447	            max: props.max.clone(),
448	            locale: env.locale.clone(),
449	            intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),
450	            messages: messages.clone(),
451	            disabled: props.disabled,
452	            readonly: props.readonly,
453	            required: props.required,
454	            name: props.name.clone(),
455	            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),
456	            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),
457	            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
458	            has_description: props.has_description,
459	            has_error_message: props.has_error_message,
460	            force_leading_zeros: props.force_leading_zeros,
461	        };
462	
463	        (State::Idle, ctx)
464	    }
465	
466	    fn transition(
467	        _state: &Self::State,
468	        event: &Self::Event,
469	        ctx: &Self::Context,
470	        _props: &Self::Props,
471	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
472	        // `SyncProps` must process even when disabled so adapter-driven prop
473	        // updates (including re-enabling) still reach the context.
474	        if ctx.disabled && !matches!(event, Event::SyncProps(_)) {
475	            return None;
476	        }
477	
478	        match event {
479	            Event::SyncProps(props) => {
480	                let props = props.as_ref().clone();
481	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
482	                    sync_props(ctx, &props);
483	                }))
484	            }
485	
486	            Event::FocusStart => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused).apply(
487	                |ctx: &mut Context| {
488	                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start);
489	                },
490	            )),
491	
492	            Event::FocusEnd => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused).apply(
493	                |ctx: &mut Context| {
494	                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End);
495	                },
496	            )),
497	
498	            Event::BlurAll => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
499	                ctx.active_field = None;
500	            })),
501	
502	            Event::SetRange(range) => {
503	                if ctx.readonly {
504	                    return None;
505	                }
506	
507	                let range = range.clone();
508	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
509	                    ctx.start_date = range.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());
510	                    ctx.end_date = range.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());
511	                    ctx.value.set(range);
512	                }))
513	            }
514	
515	            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {
516	                if ctx.readonly {
517	                    return None;
518	                }
519	
520	                let date = date.clone();
521	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
522	                    apply_start_change(ctx, date);
523	                }))
524	            }
525	
526	            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {
527	                if ctx.readonly {
528	                    return None;
529	                }
530	
531	                let date = date.clone();
532	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
533	                    apply_end_change(ctx, date);
534	                }))
535	            }
536	        }
537	    }
538	
539	    fn on_props_changed(old: &Self::Props, new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> {
540	        debug_assert_eq!(
541	            old.id, new.id,
542	            "date_range_field::Props.id must remain stable after init"
543	        );
544	
545	        if old == new {
546	            Vec::new()
547	        } else {
548	            vec![Event::SyncProps(Box::new(new.clone()))]
549	        }
550	    }
551	
552	    fn connect<'a>(
553	        state: &'a Self::State,
554	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
555	        props: &'a Self::Props,
556	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
557	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
558	        Api::new(state, ctx, props, send)
559	    }
560	}
561	
562	/// Re-derives mutable context fields from changed props.
563	///
564	/// `id` is fixed at construction (see [`Machine::on_props_changed`]). For a
565	/// controlled component (`props.value` is `Some`) the controlled override and
566	/// the per-field working values are reconciled to the new range; an
567	/// uncontrolled component keeps its internal working state and merely drops any
568	/// stale controlled override.
569	fn sync_props(ctx: &mut Context, props: &Props) {
570	    ctx.min = props.min.clone();
571	    ctx.max = props.max.clone();
572	    ctx.disabled = props.disabled;
573	    ctx.readonly = props.readonly;
574	    ctx.required = props.required;
575	    ctx.name = props.name.clone();
576	    ctx.start_name = props.start_name.clone();
577	    ctx.end_name = props.end_name.clone();
578	    ctx.has_description = props.has_description;
579	    ctx.has_error_message = props.has_error_message;
580	    ctx.force_leading_zeros = props.force_leading_zeros;
581	
582	    if let Some(controlled) = &props.value {
583	        ctx.value.sync_controlled(Some(controlled.clone()));
584	        ctx.start_date = controlled.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());
585	        ctx.end_date = controlled.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());
586	    } else {
587	        ctx.value.sync_controlled(None);
588	    }
589	}
590	
591	/// Applies a start-date change, then recomputes the derived range.
592	fn apply_start_change(ctx: &mut Context, start: Option<CalendarDate>) {
593	    ctx.start_date = start;
594	
595	    recompute_range(ctx);
596	}
597	
598	/// Applies an end-date change, then recomputes the derived range.
599	fn apply_end_change(ctx: &mut Context, end: Option<CalendarDate>) {
600	    ctx.end_date = end;
601	
602	    recompute_range(ctx);
603	}
604	
605	/// Recomputes the derived complete range from the two field values.
606	///
607	/// When both fields hold a value the range is normalized (swapping if needed so
608	/// `start <= end`) and the normalized order is reflected back into the field
609	/// values. When either field is empty the range is incomplete and the value is
610	/// cleared.
611	fn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {
612	    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {
613	        (Some(start), Some(end)) => match DateRange::normalized(start, end) {
614	            Some(range) => {
615	                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());
616	                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());
617	                ctx.value.set(Some(range));
618	            }
619	
620	            // The two dates are not comparable (e.g. different calendars/eras
621	            // supplied via the public setters); clear the derived range rather
622	            // than leave a stale value behind the new field values.
623	            None => ctx.value.set(None),
624	        },
625	
626	        _ => ctx.value.set(None),
627	    }
628	}
629	
630	/// Structural parts exposed by the `DateRangeField` connect API.
631	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
632	#[scope = "date-range-field"]
633	pub enum Part {
634	    /// The root container grouping both fields.
635	    Root,
636	
637	    /// The shared label for the entire range.
638	    Label,
639	
640	    /// The wrapper around the start `DateField`.
641	    StartField,
642	
643	    /// The visual separator between the two fields.
644	    Separator,
645	
646	    /// The wrapper around the end `DateField`.
647	    EndField,
648	
649	    /// The optional description element.
650	    Description,
651	
652	    /// The optional error message element.
653	    ErrorMessage,
654	
655	    /// The combined hidden form input.
656	    HiddenInput,
657	}

659	/// API for the `DateRangeField` component.
660	pub struct Api<'a> {
661	    /// The state of the component.
662	    state: &'a State,
663	
664	    /// The context of the component.
665	    ctx: &'a Context,
666	
667	    /// The props of the component.
668	    props: &'a Props,
669	
670	    /// The send function.
671	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
672	}
673	
674	impl Debug for Api<'_> {
675	    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
676	        f.debug_struct("Api")
677	            .field("state", self.state)
678	            .field("ctx", self.ctx)
679	            .field("props", self.props)
680	            .finish_non_exhaustive()
681	    }
682	}

684	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
685	    /// Creates a `DateRangeField` connect API from machine state.
686	    #[must_use]
687	    pub const fn new(
688	        state: &'a State,
689	        ctx: &'a Context,
690	        props: &'a Props,
691	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),

701	    // ── AttrMap getters ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

703	    /// Returns attributes for the root element.
704	    #[must_use]
705	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
706	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
707	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
708	
709	        attrs
710	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
711	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
712	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id())
713	            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, "group")
714	            .set(
715	                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy),
716	                self.ctx.ids.part("label"),
717	            )
718	            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_name());
719	
720	        let mut described_by = Vec::new();
721	
722	        if self.ctx.has_description {
723	            described_by.push(self.ctx.ids.part("description"));
724	        }
725	
726	        if self.ctx.has_error_message {
727	            described_by.push(self.ctx.ids.part("error-message"));
728	        }
729	
730	        if !described_by.is_empty() {
731	            attrs.set(
732	                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy),
733	                described_by.join(" "),
734	            );
735	        }
736	
737	        if self.ctx.disabled {
738	            attrs
739	                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true)
740	                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), "true");
741	        }
742	
743	        if self.ctx.readonly {
744	            attrs
745	                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-readonly"), true)
746	                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), "true");
747	        }
748	
749	        if self.ctx.required {
750	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Required), "true");
751	        }
752	
753	        if self.ctx.is_invalid() {
754	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-invalid"), true);
755	        }
756	
757	        attrs
758	    }
759

822	    /// Returns a screen-reader description of the full range (e.g. "March 1,
823	    /// 2025 to March 15, 2025"), or `None` if no complete range is selected.
824	    #[must_use]
825	    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {
826	        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;
827	
828	        let start = format_date_label(
829	            &range.start,
830	            self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(),
831	            &self.ctx.locale,
832	        );
833	
834	        let end = format_date_label(&range.end, self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(), &self.ctx.locale);
835	
836	        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(
837	            &start,
838	            &end,
839	            &self.ctx.locale,
840	        ))
841	    }
842	
843	    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the start date input.
844	    ///
845	    /// Both child fields are bounded only by the global `min`/`max`, never by
846	    /// the opposite endpoint: a child `DateField` clamps completed dates to its
847	    /// own `max_value`/`min_value`, so binding the start field to the current
848	    /// end date would clamp away an out-of-order edit before the parent can
849	    /// perform the documented swap. Cross-field ordering is enforced by
850	    /// [`recompute_range`] when the changed value reaches the parent, not by the
851	    /// child's clamp. `invalid` reflects whether this field's own value lies
852	    /// outside the global bounds.
853	    #[must_use]
854	    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
855	        date_field::Props {
856	            id: self.ctx.ids.part("start"),
857	            value: Some(self.ctx.start_date.clone()),
858	            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),
859	            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),
860	            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
861	            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
862	            required: self.ctx.required,
863	            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(
864	                self.ctx.start_date.as_ref(),
865	                self.ctx.min.as_ref(),
866	                self.ctx.max.as_ref(),
867	            ),
868	            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
869	            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,
870	            ..date_field::Props::default()
871	        }
872	    }
873	
874	    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the end date input.
875	    ///
876	    /// Bounded only by the global `min`/`max` for the same reason as
877	    /// [`Api::start_field_props`]; `invalid` reflects whether the end field's
878	    /// own value lies outside the global bounds.
879	    #[must_use]
880	    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
881	        date_field::Props {
882	            id: self.ctx.ids.part("end"),
883	            value: Some(self.ctx.end_date.clone()),
884	            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),
885	            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),
886	            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
887	            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
888	            required: self.ctx.required,
889	            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(
890	                self.ctx.end_date.as_ref(),
891	                self.ctx.min.as_ref(),
892	                self.ctx.max.as_ref(),
893	            ),
894	            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
895	            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,
896	            ..date_field::Props::default()
897	        }
898	    }

929	    /// Returns attributes for the combined hidden input element.
930	    ///
931	    /// The value is the ISO 8601 interval `YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD`, or empty when
932	    /// no range is selected.
933	    #[must_use]
934	    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
935	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
936	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();
937	
938	        attrs
939	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
940	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
941	            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
942	
943	        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {
944	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
945	        }
946	
947	        // A disabled control is excluded from form submission; mark it disabled
948	        // rather than submit a stale value (mirrors `date_picker`).
949	        if self.ctx.disabled {
950	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
951	        }
952	
953	        let value = if let Some(range) = self.ctx.value.get() {
954	            range.to_iso8601()
955	        } else {
956	            String::new()
957	        };
958	
959	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);
960	
961	        attrs
962	    }
963	
964	    /// Returns attributes for the separate hidden input carrying the start date.
965	    ///
966	    /// Only meaningful when `start_name` is set on `Props`. The value is the
967	    /// start field's own date (which may be set before a complete range
968	    /// exists), so a partially-entered range still submits the start date.
969	    #[must_use]
970	    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
971	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
972	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
973	
974	        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {
975	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
976	        }
977	
978	        if self.ctx.disabled {
979	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
980	        }
981	
982	        let value = match &self.ctx.start_date {
983	            Some(start) => start.to_iso8601(),
984	            None => String::new(),
985	        };
986	
987	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);
988	
989	        attrs
990	    }
991	
992	    /// Returns attributes for the separate hidden input carrying the end date.
993	    ///
994	    /// Only meaningful when `end_name` is set on `Props`. The value is the end
995	    /// field's own date, so a partially-entered range still submits the end
996	    /// date.
997	    #[must_use]
998	    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
999	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
1000	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
1001	
1002	        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {
1003	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
1004	        }
1005	
1006	        if self.ctx.disabled {
1007	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
1008	        }
1009	
1010	        let value = match &self.ctx.end_date {
1011	            Some(end) => end.to_iso8601(),
1012	            None => String::new(),
1013	        };

1015	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);

1017	        attrs

1089	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
1090	    type Part = Part;

1093	        match part {
1094	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
1095	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
1096	            Part::StartField => self.start_field_attrs(),
1097	            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),
1098	            Part::EndField => self.end_field_attrs(),
1099	            Part::Description => self.description_attrs(),
1100	            Part::ErrorMessage => self.error_message_attrs(),
1101	            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),
1102	        }
1103	    }
1104	}

1106	/// Formats a single date as a human-readable label for screen-reader range
1107	/// descriptions (e.g. "March 1, 2025").
1108	fn format_date_label(date: &CalendarDate, backend: &dyn IntlBackend, locale: &Locale) -> String {
1109	    format!(
1110	        "{} {}, {}",
1111	        backend.month_long_name(date.month(), locale),
1112	        date.day(),
1113	        date.year(),
1114	    )
1115	}

1	//! `DatePicker` component state machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! Framework-agnostic implementation of the composite date picker defined in
4	//! `spec/components/date-time/date-picker.md`. The machine pairs a date text
5	//! input with an embedded [`Calendar`](super::calendar) shown in a popover and
6	//! owns the popover open/close lifecycle, the selected value, input
7	//! parsing/validation, the value↔calendar synchronisation point, and the
8	//! ARIA/`data-ars-*` attribute surface.
9	//!
10	//! ## Spec-vs-implementation reconciliation
11	//!
12	//! The spec's §1.7/§1.8 code examples predate the current core engine and
13	//! sibling components. This implementation keeps the spec's semantics while
14	//! using the real APIs:
15	//!
16	//! - **Named effects, not platform calls.** Focus is adapter-driven. The
17	//!   machine declares a typed [`Effect`] enum and emits
18	//!   [`PendingEffect::named`](ars_core::PendingEffect::named) intents
19	//!   ([`Effect::FocusCalendar`], [`Effect::RestoreFocusToTrigger`],
20	//!   [`Effect::RestoreFocusToInput`]); the adapter performs the DOM focus from
21	//!   live element handles. This matches `popover`/`dialog` and the issue's
22	//!   element/ref handling note. The spec's `use_platform_effects()` /
23	//!   `focus_element_by_id` closures are not used in core.
24	//! - **Real [`CalendarDate`] API.** Year/month/day are `year()`/`month()`/
25	//!   `day()` methods returning plain integers, `new_gregorian` is fallible, and
26	//!   `CalendarDate` has no `Ord` — range checks use
27	//!   [`CalendarDate::compare`].
28	//! - **Forwarded predicate type.** `is_date_unavailable` reuses
29	//!   [`calendar::IsDateUnavailableFn`] so it forwards into [`Api::calendar_props`].
30	//! - **Controlled-prop sync.** A [`Event::SyncProps`] event plus
31	//!   [`Machine::on_props_changed`](ars_core::Machine::on_props_changed) and
32	//!   [`Machine::initial_effects`](ars_core::Machine::initial_effects) keep
33	//!   controlled `value`/`open`/`min`/`max`/`disabled` live, matching
34	//!   Calendar/DateField/Popover.
35	//! - **Format-aware parse/format.** [`format_date`]/[`parse_date`] honour the
36	//!   resolved `format` pattern (field order + separator) instead of a hardcoded
37	//!   `MM/dd/yyyy` placeholder.
38	
39	#[cfg(test)]
40	mod tests;
41

43	    boxed::Box,
44	    format,
45	    string::{String, ToString as _},
46	    vec,
47	    vec::Vec,
48	};
49	use core::{
50	    cmp::Ordering,
51	    fmt::{self, Debug, Write as _},
52	};
53	
54	use ars_core::{
55	    AriaAttr, AttrMap, Bindable, Callback, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart,
56	    ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, TransitionPlan,
57	};
58	use ars_i18n::{CalendarDate, DateOrder, date_field_separator, date_order, normalize_digits};
59	use ars_interactions::{KeyboardEventData, KeyboardKey};
60	
61	use super::calendar;

63	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
64	// State / Event / Effect
65	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
66	
67	/// States for the `DatePicker` component.
68	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
69	pub enum State {
70	    /// Popover is closed; the input field may or may not have focus.
71	    Closed,
72	
73	    /// Popover is open and the calendar is visible.
74	    Open,
75	}
76	
77	/// Events for the `DatePicker` component.
78	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
79	pub enum Event {
80	    /// Open the calendar popover.
81	    Open,
82	
83	    /// Close the calendar popover.
84	    Close,
85	
86	    /// Toggle the popover.
87	    Toggle,
88	
89	    /// A date was selected from the embedded calendar.
90	    SelectDate {
91	        /// The newly selected date.
92	        date: CalendarDate,
93	    },
94	
95	    /// The text input value changed (user typing in the field).
96	    InputChange {
97	        /// The raw text now present in the input.
98	        value: String,
99	    },
100	
101	    /// Focus entered the input field (the date field). With `open_on_click`,
102	    /// this opens the calendar. Adapters dispatch it from input focus only — not
103	    /// trigger focus, which the trigger's own click/keydown handlers manage.
104	    FocusIn,
105	
106	    /// Focus left the date picker entirely.
107	    FocusOut,
108	
109	    /// Keyboard event on the input or trigger.
110	    KeyDown {
111	        /// The key that was pressed.
112	        key: KeyboardKey,
113	    },
114	
115	    /// Re-apply context-backed prop fields after a props change.
116	    ///
117	    /// Emitted by [`Machine::on_props_changed`](ars_core::Machine::on_props_changed)
118	    /// so controlled `value`/`open` and the cached `min`/`max`/`disabled`/
119	    /// `format` fields follow parent-driven prop updates.
120	    SyncProps(Box<Props>),
121	}
122	
123	/// Typed identifier for every named effect intent the `date_picker` machine
124	/// emits.
125	///
126	/// Each variant is a stable identifier the adapter routes on with an
127	/// exhaustive `match` to perform the live, element-handle-based focus
128	/// operation the agnostic core cannot perform itself.

130	pub enum Effect {
131	    /// Notify the consumer that the open state changed. Emitted on every
132	    /// open ↔ closed transition (and on a non-closed initial mount). The
133	    /// adapter reads the current open state from the connect API and invokes
134	    /// [`Props::on_open_change`] with it — the channel a controlled-`open`
135	    /// parent uses to reconcile after a user-driven open/close.
136	    OpenChange,
137	
138	    /// Notify the consumer that the selected value changed. Emitted whenever a
139	    /// selection, accepted typed entry, or clear changes the requested value.
140	    /// The adapter reads [`Context::requested_value`] (the requested date — not
141	    /// `value.get()`, which a controlled bindable holds at the parent's value)
142	    /// and forwards it, so a controlled-`value` parent learns which date to feed
143	    /// back. Mirrors the `requested_value` + `ValueChange` convention used by
144	    /// other value-bearing components (e.g. `rating_group`).
145	    ValueChange,
146	
147	    /// Move focus into the embedded calendar (its grid, falling back to the
148	    /// content container). Emitted when the popover opens.
149	    FocusCalendar,
150	
151	    /// Return focus to the trigger button. Emitted when the popover closes via
152	    /// [`Event::Close`] or an Escape key press.
153	    RestoreFocusToTrigger,
154	
155	    /// Return focus to the text input. Emitted when a calendar selection closes
156	    /// the popover (`close_on_select`).
157	    RestoreFocusToInput,
158	}
159

162	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
163	
164	/// Props for the `DatePicker` component.
165	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
166	pub struct Props {
167	    /// The ID of the date picker. The adapter supplies a hydration-stable base;
168	    /// [`ComponentIds`] derives part IDs from it.
169	    pub id: String,
170	
171	    /// Controlled date value. `None` = uncontrolled, `Some(None)` =
172	    /// controlled-and-empty, `Some(Some(date))` = controlled with a date.
173	    pub value: Option<Option<CalendarDate>>,
174	
175	    /// Default date for uncontrolled mode.
176	    pub default_value: Option<CalendarDate>,
177	
178	    /// Minimum selectable date (forwarded to the embedded Calendar).
179	    pub min: Option<CalendarDate>,
180	
181	    /// Maximum selectable date (forwarded to the embedded Calendar).
182	    pub max: Option<CalendarDate>,
183	
184	    /// Whether the date picker is non-interactive.
185	    pub disabled: bool,
186	
187	    /// Whether the date picker allows viewing but not editing.
188	    pub readonly: bool,
189	
190	    /// Predicate for unavailable dates (forwarded to the embedded Calendar).
191	    pub is_date_unavailable: Option<calendar::IsDateUnavailableFn>,
192	
193	    /// Date format pattern. Defaults to a locale-appropriate format.
194	    pub format: Option<String>,
195	
196	    /// Placeholder text for the input field.
197	    pub placeholder: Option<String>,
198	
199	    /// Form field name for hidden input submission.
200	    pub name: Option<String>,
201	
202	    /// Whether the field is required.
203	    pub required: bool,
204	
205	    /// Right-to-left layout direction.
206	    pub is_rtl: bool,
207	
208	    /// Label text.
209	    pub label: String,
210	
211	    /// Description/help text.
212	    pub description: Option<String>,
213	
214	    /// Error message.
215	    pub error_message: Option<String>,
216	
217	    /// Whether the field is in an invalid state.
218	    pub invalid: bool,
219	
220	    /// Whether to close the popover after a date is selected. Default: `true`.
221	    pub close_on_select: bool,
222	
223	    /// Controlled open state. `Some(true)` = forced open, `Some(false)` =
224	    /// forced closed, `None` = uncontrolled.
225	    pub open: Option<bool>,
226	
227	    /// Default open state for uncontrolled mode.
228	    pub default_open: bool,
229	
230	    /// When `true` (the default), clicking or focusing the date field opens the
231	    /// calendar popover. When `false`, only the trigger button opens it.
232	    pub open_on_click: bool,
233	
234	    /// Number of months to display side-by-side in the calendar popover.
235	    /// Default: `1`. Forwarded to the embedded Calendar's `visible_months`.
236	    pub visible_months: usize,
237	
238	    /// The "today" date, injected by the adapter for testability and SSR
239	    /// determinism. Forwarded to the embedded Calendar's `today` so an empty
240	    /// picker opens on the current month and marks the correct day. Defaults to
241	    /// a fixed date (matching `calendar::Props::default().today`); adapters
242	    /// inject the real today.
243	    pub today: CalendarDate,
244	
245	    /// Called whenever the open state changes. Fired by the adapter from the
246	    /// [`Effect::OpenChange`] intent with the new open value. A controlled-`open`
247	    /// parent uses this to reconcile its state after a user-driven open/close
248	    /// (mirrors `popover`/`dialog`).
249	    pub on_open_change: Option<Callback<dyn Fn(bool) + Send + Sync>>,
250	
251	    /// Called whenever the selected value changes. Fired by the adapter from the
252	    /// [`Effect::ValueChange`] intent with [`Context::requested_value`] — the
253	    /// requested date, or `None` for a clear. A controlled-`value` parent uses
254	    /// this to echo the new value back (the value counterpart of
255	    /// [`on_open_change`](Self::on_open_change)); without it a controlled parent
256	    /// has no channel to learn the user's selection or clear.
257	    pub on_value_change: Option<Callback<dyn Fn(Option<CalendarDate>) + Send + Sync>>,
258	}

1	//! Unit and snapshot tests for the `DateRangeField` component.
2	//!
3	//! Test names that begin with `snapshot_` use `insta::assert_snapshot!` and
4	//! commit golden output under `snapshots/`. Every other test is a pure

6	//! files.

8	use alloc::{boxed::Box, format, string::String, sync::Arc, vec::Vec};

11	// `Machine as _` brings the `ars_core::Machine` trait into scope so
12	// `Machine::on_props_changed` resolves, without shadowing the local `Machine`.

14	    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr, Machine as _, Service,

27	}

45	    Service::<Machine>::new(props(), &env(en_us()), &Messages::default())
46	}
47

50	}

66	    service.connect(&|_| {}).field_group_attrs()
67	}

#[test]
fn controlled_value_update_syncs_after_mount() {
    let mut svc = service_with(
        Props {
            value: Some(Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15)))),
            name: Some(String::from("trip-range")),
            ..props()
        },
        en_us(),
    );

    drop(svc.set_props(Props {
        value: Some(Some(range(date(2025, 9, 1), date(2025, 9, 30)))),
        name: Some(String::from("trip-range")),
        ..props()
    }));

    let ctx = svc.context();
    assert_eq!(ctx.start_date, Some(date(2025, 9, 1)));
    assert_eq!(ctx.end_date, Some(date(2025, 9, 30)));
    assert_eq!(
        *ctx.value.get(),
        Some(range(date(2025, 9, 1), date(2025, 9, 30)))
    );

    assert_eq!(
        attr(&svc.connect(&|_| {}).hidden_input_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Value).as_deref(),
        Some("2025-09-01/2025-09-30")
    );
}

    pub fn row_attrs(&self, week_index: usize) -> AttrMap {
        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] =
            Part::Row { week_index }.data_attrs();

        attrs
            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
            .set(part_attr, part_val)
            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-week-index"), week_index.to_string());

        attrs
    }
